  u_2} : (n : Nat) →

    (K : Type u_2) →

                @Eq.{1} Nat

                      (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_1} F
                        (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst)))

                        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                          (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1))))

                        (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
                        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                          (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))
                        inst_4))

                  @LE.le.{0} Nat instLENat (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 2) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 2))) n → Prop

STATEMENT Atlas.Unitary.PSU SOURCE Atlas.LinearGroups.Unitary.Basic
Atlas.Unitary.PSU.{u_1,
  u_2} : (F : Type u_1) →
  (K : Type u_2) →
    [inst : Field.{u_1} F] →
      [inst_1 : Field.{u_2} K] →
        [Finite.{u_1 + 1} F] →
          [Finite.{u_2 + 1} K] →
            [inst_4 :
                @Algebra.{u_1, u_2} F K (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
                  (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                    (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))] →
              Nat →
                @Eq.{1} Nat
                    (@Module.finrank.{u_1, u_2} F K
                      (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_1} F
                        (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst)))
                      (@Semiring.toAddCommMonoid.{u_2} K
                        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                          (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1))))
                      (@Algebra.toModule.{u_1, u_2} F K
                        (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
                        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                          (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))
                        inst_4))
                    (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 2) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 2))) →
                  Type u_2
STATEMENT Atlas.Unitary.card_center SOURCE Atlas.LinearGroups.Unitary.Center
@Atlas.Unitary.card_center.{u_1,
    u_2} : ∀ {F : Type u_1} {K : Type u_2} [inst : Field.{u_1} F] [inst_1 : Field.{u_2} K] [inst_2 : Finite.{u_1 + 1} F]
  [inst_3 : Finite.{u_2 + 1} K]
  [inst_4 :
    @Algebra.{u_1, u_2} F K (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
      (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
        (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))]
  {n : Nat}
  (hdeg :
    @Eq.{1} Nat
      (@Module.finrank.{u_1, u_2} F K
        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_1} F (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst)))
        (@Semiring.toAddCommMonoid.{u_2} K
          (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
            (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1))))
        (@Algebra.toModule.{u_1, u_2} F K (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
          (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
            (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))
          inst_4))
      (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 2) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 2)))),
  @LT.lt.{0} Nat instLTNat (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 0) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 0))) n →
    @Eq.{1} Nat
      (Nat.card.{u_2}
        ↥(@Subgroup.center.{u_2} (@Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
            (@Atlas.Unitary.group_su.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)))
      (n.gcd
        (@HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} Nat Nat Nat (@instHAdd.{0} Nat instAddNat) (Nat.card.{u_1} F)
          (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 1) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 1)))))

@Atlas.exists_type2A.{u_1,
    u_2} : ∀ {F : Type u_1} {K : Type u_2} [inst : Field.{u_1} F] [inst_1 : Field.{u_2} K] [inst_2 : Finite.{u_1 + 1} F]

  [inst_4 :
    @Algebra.{u_1, u_2} F K (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
      (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
        (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))]
  (hdeg :
    @Eq.{1} Nat
      (@Module.finrank.{u_1, u_2} F K
        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_1} F (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst)))
        (@Semiring.toAddCommMonoid.{u_2} K
          (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
            (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1))))
        (@Algebra.toModule.{u_1, u_2} F K (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
          (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
            (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))
          inst_4))
      (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 2) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 2))))
  (n : Nat) (hn : @LE.le.{0} Nat instLENat (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 2) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 2))) n),
  Atlas.Unitary.Good n (Nat.card.{u_1} F) →
    ∃ G x,
      And (Finite.{u_2 + 1} G)
        (And (@IsSimpleGroup.{u_2} G x)
          (And
            (Not
              (@IsMulCommutative.{u_2} G
                (@MulOne.toMul.{u_2} G
                  (@MulOneClass.toMulOne.{u_2} G
                    (@Monoid.toMulOneClass.{u_2} G
                      (@DivInvMonoid.toMonoid.{u_2} G (@Group.toDivInvMonoid.{u_2} G x)))))))
            (And
              (@Eq.{1} Nat (Nat.card.{u_2} G)
                (@HDiv.hDiv.{0, 0, 0} Nat Nat Nat (@instHDiv.{0} Nat Nat.instDiv)
                  (Atlas.Unitary.orderSU (Nat.card.{u_1} F) n)
                  (n.gcd
                    (@HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} Nat Nat Nat (@instHAdd.{0} Nat instAddNat) (Nat.card.{u_1} F)
                      (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 1) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 1)))))))
              (And
                (Nonempty.{u_2 + 1}
                  (@MulEquiv.{u_2, u_2} G (@Atlas.Unitary.PSU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
                    (@MulOne.toMul.{u_2} G
                      (@MulOneClass.toMulOne.{u_2} G
                        (@Monoid.toMulOneClass.{u_2} G
                          (@DivInvMonoid.toMonoid.{u_2} G (@Group.toDivInvMonoid.{u_2} G x)))))
                    (@MulOne.toMul.{u_2} (@Atlas.Unitary.PSU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
                      (@MulOneClass.toMulOne.{u_2}
                        (@Atlas.Unitary.PSU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
                        (@Monoid.toMulOneClass.{u_2}
                          (@Atlas.Unitary.PSU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
                          (@DivInvMonoid.toMonoid.{u_2}
                            (@Atlas.Unitary.PSU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
                            (@Group.toDivInvMonoid.{u_2}
                              (@Atlas.Unitary.PSU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
                              (@QuotientGroup.Quotient.group.{u_2}
                                (@Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
                                (@Atlas.Unitary.group_su.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
                                (@Subgroup.center.{u_2}
                                  (@Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
                                  (@Atlas.Unitary.group_su.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg))
                                ⋯))))))))
                (@Atlas.Type2AConstruction.{u_1, u_2} n F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 hdeg hn)))))
STATEMENT Atlas.type2A_construction SOURCE Atlas.LinearGroups.Type2A
@Atlas.type2A_construction.{u_1,
    u_2} : ∀ {F : Type u_1} {K : Type u_2} [inst : Field.{u_1} F] [inst_1 : Field.{u_2} K] [inst_2 : Finite.{u_1 + 1} F]
  [inst_3 : Finite.{u_2 + 1} K]
  [inst_4 :
    @Algebra.{u_1, u_2} F K (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
      (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
        (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))]
  (hdeg :
    @Eq.{1} Nat
      (@Module.finrank.{u_1, u_2} F K
        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_1} F (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst)))
        (@Semiring.toAddCommMonoid.{u_2} K
          (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
            (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1))))
        (@Algebra.toModule.{u_1, u_2} F K (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
          (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
            (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))
          inst_4))
      (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 2) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 2))))
  (n : Nat) (hn : @LE.le.{0} Nat instLENat (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 2) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 2))) n),
  Atlas.Unitary.Good n (Nat.card.{u_1} F) →
    @Atlas.Type2AConstruction.{u_1, u_2} n F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 hdeg hn
STATEMENT Atlas.type2A_prime_power SOURCE Atlas.LinearGroups.Type2A
Atlas.type2A_prime_power : ∀ (p f n : Nat),
  Nat.Prime p →
    @LE.le.{0} Nat instLENat (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 1) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 1))) f →
      ∀ (hn : @LE.le.{0} Nat instLENat (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 2) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 2))) n),
        Atlas.Unitary.Good n
            (@HPow.hPow.{0, 0, 0} Nat Nat Nat
              (@instHPow.{0, 0} Nat Nat (@NPow.toPow.{0} Nat (@Monoid.toNPow.{0} Nat Nat.instMonoid))) p f) →
          ∃ F K x x_1,
            ∃ (x_2 : Finite.{1} F) (x_3 : Finite.{1} K),
              ∃ x_4,
                ∃ (hdeg :
                  @Eq.{1} Nat
                    (@Module.finrank.{0, 0} F K
                      (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{0} F
                        (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{0} F (@Field.toSemifield.{0} F x)))
                      (@Semiring.toAddCommMonoid.{0} K
                        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{0} K
                          (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{0} K (@Field.toSemifield.{0} K x_1))))
                      (@Algebra.toModule.{0, 0} F K (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{0} F (@Field.toSemifield.{0} F x))
                        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{0} K
                          (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{0} K (@Field.toSemifield.{0} K x_1)))
                        x_4))
                    (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 2) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 2)))),
                  And
                    (@Eq.{1} Nat (Nat.card.{0} F)
                      (@HPow.hPow.{0, 0, 0} Nat Nat Nat
                        (@instHPow.{0, 0} Nat Nat (@NPow.toPow.{0} Nat (@Monoid.toNPow.{0} Nat Nat.instMonoid))) p f))
                    (And
                      (@Eq.{1} Nat (Nat.card.{0} K)
                        (@HPow.hPow.{0, 0, 0} Nat Nat Nat
                          (@instHPow.{0, 0} Nat Nat (@NPow.toPow.{0} Nat (@Monoid.toNPow.{0} Nat Nat.instMonoid))) p
                          (@HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} Nat Nat Nat (@instHMul.{0} Nat instMulNat)
                            (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 2) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 2))) f)))
                      (@Atlas.Type2AConstruction.{0, 0} n F K x x_1 x_2 x_3 x_4 hdeg hn))

                      (@Atlas.Unitary.group_su.{u_1, u_2} F K inst✝ inst✝¹ inst✝³ inst✝⁴ inst✝² n hdeg)
                      (@Subgroup.center.{u_2}
                        (@Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst✝ inst✝¹ inst✝³ inst✝⁴ inst✝² n hdeg)
                        (@Atlas.Unitary.group_su.{u_1, u_2} F K inst✝ inst✝¹ inst✝³ inst✝⁴ inst✝² n hdeg))
                      ⋯))))))
          (@Atlas.Unitary.projection.{u_1, u_2} F K inst✝ inst✝¹ inst✝³ inst✝⁴ inst✝² n hdeg)))
    (projection_kernel :
      @Eq.{u_2 + 1}
        (@Subgroup.{u_2} (@Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst✝ inst✝¹ inst✝³ inst✝⁴ inst✝² n hdeg)

DEFINITION Atlas.Unitary.Good SOURCE Atlas.LinearGroups.Unitary.Basic
def Atlas.Unitary.Good : Nat → Nat → Prop :=
fun n q =>
  And (@LE.le.{0} Nat instLENat (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 2) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 2))) n)
    (And
      (@Ne.{1} (Prod.{0, 0} Nat Nat) (@Prod.mk.{0, 0} Nat Nat n q)
        (@Prod.mk.{0, 0} Nat Nat (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 2) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 2)))
          (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 2) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 2)))))
      (And
        (@Ne.{1} (Prod.{0, 0} Nat Nat) (@Prod.mk.{0, 0} Nat Nat n q)
          (@Prod.mk.{0, 0} Nat Nat (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 2) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 2)))
            (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 3) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 3)))))
        (@Ne.{1} (Prod.{0, 0} Nat Nat) (@Prod.mk.{0, 0} Nat Nat n q)
          (@Prod.mk.{0, 0} Nat Nat (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 3) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 3)))
            (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 2) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 2)))))))
DEFINITION Atlas.Unitary.group_su SOURCE Atlas.LinearGroups.Unitary.Basic
@[instance_reducible] def Atlas.Unitary.group_su.{u_1, u_2} : (F : Type u_1) →
  (K : Type u_2) →
    [inst : Field.{u_1} F] →
      [inst_1 : Field.{u_2} K] →
        [inst_2 : Finite.{u_1 + 1} F] →
          [inst_3 : Finite.{u_2 + 1} K] →
            [inst_4 :
                @Algebra.{u_1, u_2} F K (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
                  (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                    (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))] →
              (n : Nat) →
                (hdeg :
                    @Eq.{1} Nat
                      (@Module.finrank.{u_1, u_2} F K
                        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_1} F
                          (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst)))
                        (@Semiring.toAddCommMonoid.{u_2} K
                          (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                            (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1))))
                        (@Algebra.toModule.{u_1, u_2} F K
                          (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
                          (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                            (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))
                          inst_4))
                      (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 2) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 2)))) →
                  Group.{u_2} (@Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg) :=
fun F K [inst : Field.{u_1} F] [inst_1 : Field.{u_2} K] [inst_2 : Finite.{u_1 + 1} F] [inst_3 : Finite.{u_2 + 1} K]
    [inst_4 :
      @Algebra.{u_1, u_2} F K (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
          (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))]
    n hdeg =>
  @id.{u_2 + 1} (Group.{u_2} (@Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg))
    (@inferInstance.{u_2 + 1}
      (Group.{u_2}
        ↥(@Matrix.specialUnitaryGroup.{0, u_2} (Fin n) (instDecidableEqFin n) (Fin.fintype n) K
            (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1)
            (@Atlas.Unitary.conjugationStarRing.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 hdeg)))
      (@Matrix.instGroupSubtypeMemSubmonoidSpecialUnitaryGroup.{0, u_2} (Fin n) (instDecidableEqFin n) (Fin.fintype n) K
        (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1)
        (@Atlas.Unitary.conjugationStarRing.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 hdeg)))
DEFINITION Atlas.Unitary.conjugationStarRing SOURCE Atlas.LinearGroups.Unitary.Basic
@[instance_reducible] def Atlas.Unitary.conjugationStarRing.{u_1, u_2} : (F : Type u_1) →
  (K : Type u_2) →
    [inst : Field.{u_1} F] →
      [inst_1 : Field.{u_2} K] →
        [Finite.{u_1 + 1} F] →
          [Finite.{u_2 + 1} K] →
            [inst_4 :
                @Algebra.{u_1, u_2} F K (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
                  (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                    (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))] →
              @Eq.{1} Nat
                  (@Module.finrank.{u_1, u_2} F K
                    (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_1} F
                      (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst)))
                    (@Semiring.toAddCommMonoid.{u_2} K
                      (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                        (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1))))
                    (@Algebra.toModule.{u_1, u_2} F K
                      (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
                      (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                        (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))
                      inst_4))
                  (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 2) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 2))) →
                @StarRing.{u_2} K
                  (@NonUnitalNonAssocRing.toNonUnitalNonAssocSemiring.{u_2} K

                      (@NonUnitalCommRing.toNonUnitalNonAssocCommRing.{u_2} K
                        (@CommRing.toNonUnitalCommRing.{u_2} K (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1))))) :=

    [inst_4 :
      @Algebra.{u_1, u_2} F K (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
          (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))]
    hdeg =>
  @StarRing.mk.{u_2} K
    (@NonUnitalNonAssocRing.toNonUnitalNonAssocSemiring.{u_2} K
      (@NonUnitalNonAssocCommRing.toNonUnitalNonAssocRing.{u_2} K
        (@NonUnitalCommRing.toNonUnitalNonAssocCommRing.{u_2} K
          (@CommRing.toNonUnitalCommRing.{u_2} K (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1)))))
    (@StarMul.mk.{u_2} K
      (@Distrib.toMul.{u_2} K
        (@NonUnitalNonAssocSemiring.toDistrib.{u_2} K
          (@NonUnitalNonAssocRing.toNonUnitalNonAssocSemiring.{u_2} K
            (@NonUnitalNonAssocCommRing.toNonUnitalNonAssocRing.{u_2} K
              (@NonUnitalCommRing.toNonUnitalNonAssocCommRing.{u_2} K
                (@CommRing.toNonUnitalCommRing.{u_2} K (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1)))))))
      (@InvolutiveStar.mk.{u_2} K
        (@Star.mk.{u_2} K
          (@DFunLike.coe.{u_2 + 1, u_2 + 1, u_2 + 1}
            (@AlgEquiv.{u_1, u_2, u_2} F K K (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
              (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))
              (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))
              inst_4 inst_4)
            K (fun x => K)
            (@AlgEquiv.instFunLike.{u_1, u_2, u_2} F K K
              (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
              (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))
              (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))
              inst_4 inst_4)
            (@Atlas.FiniteQuadratic.conjugation.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4)))
        ⋯)
      ⋯)
    ⋯

                        inst_4))

DEFINITION Atlas.Unitary.orderSU SOURCE Atlas.LinearGroups.Unitary.OrderFormula
def Atlas.Unitary.orderSU : Nat → Nat → Nat :=
fun q n =>
  @HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} Nat Nat Nat (@instHMul.{0} Nat instMulNat)
    (@HPow.hPow.{0, 0, 0} Nat Nat Nat
      (@instHPow.{0, 0} Nat Nat (@NPow.toPow.{0} Nat (@Monoid.toNPow.{0} Nat Nat.instMonoid))) q
      (∑ i ∈ Finset.range n, i))
    (∏
      i ∈
        Finset.range
          (@HSub.hSub.{0, 0, 0} Nat Nat Nat (@instHSub.{0} Nat instSubNat) n
            (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 1) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 1)))),
      Atlas.Unitary.orderFactor q
        (@HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} Nat Nat Nat (@instHAdd.{0} Nat instAddNat) i
          (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 2) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 2)))))
DEFINITION Atlas.Unitary.orderFactor SOURCE Atlas.LinearGroups.Unitary.Basic
def Atlas.Unitary.orderFactor : Nat → Nat → Nat :=
fun q i =>
  @ite.{1} Nat (@Even.{0} Nat instAddNat i) i.instDecidablePredEven
    (@HSub.hSub.{0, 0, 0} Nat Nat Nat (@instHSub.{0} Nat instSubNat)
      (@HPow.hPow.{0, 0, 0} Nat Nat Nat
        (@instHPow.{0, 0} Nat Nat (@NPow.toPow.{0} Nat (@Monoid.toNPow.{0} Nat Nat.instMonoid))) q i)
      (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 1) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 1))))
    (@HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} Nat Nat Nat (@instHAdd.{0} Nat instAddNat)
      (@HPow.hPow.{0, 0, 0} Nat Nat Nat
        (@instHPow.{0, 0} Nat Nat (@NPow.toPow.{0} Nat (@Monoid.toNPow.{0} Nat Nat.instMonoid))) q i)
      (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 1) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 1))))

def Atlas.Unitary.transvectionGenerated.{u_1, u_2} : {F : Type u_1} →

    [inst : Field.{u_1} F] →
      [inst_1 : Field.{u_2} K] →

            [inst_4 :

                  (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                    (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))] →
              {n : Nat} →
                (hdeg :
                    @Eq.{1} Nat
                      (@Module.finrank.{u_1, u_2} F K
                        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_1} F
                          (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst)))

                          (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                            (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1))))
                        (@Algebra.toModule.{u_1, u_2} F K
                          (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
                          (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                            (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))
                          inst_4))
                      (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 2) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 2)))) →
                  @Subgroup.{u_2} (@Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
                    (@Atlas.Unitary.group_su.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg) :=
fun {F} {K} [inst : Field.{u_1} F] [inst_1 : Field.{u_2} K] [inst_2 : Finite.{u_1 + 1} F] [inst_3 : Finite.{u_2 + 1} K]
    [inst_4 :
      @Algebra.{u_1, u_2} F K (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
          (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))]
    {n} hdeg =>
  ⨆ v,
    ⨆ (hv :
      @Eq.{u_2 + 1} K (@Atlas.Unitary.hermitian.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n v v)
        (@OfNat.ofNat.{u_2} K (nat_lit 0)
          (@Zero.toOfNat0.{u_2} K
            (@MulZeroClass.toZero.{u_2} K
              (@instMulZeroClassOfSemiring.{u_2} K
                (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                  (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))))))),
      @Atlas.Unitary.lineTransvections.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg v hv
DEFINITION Atlas.Unitary.lineTransvections SOURCE Atlas.LinearGroups.Unitary.LineTransvections
def Atlas.Unitary.lineTransvections.{u_1, u_2} : {F : Type u_1} →
  {K : Type u_2} →
    [inst : Field.{u_1} F] →
      [inst_1 : Field.{u_2} K] →
        [inst_2 : Finite.{u_1 + 1} F] →
          [inst_3 : Finite.{u_2 + 1} K] →
            [inst_4 :
                @Algebra.{u_1, u_2} F K (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
                  (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                    (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))] →
              {n : Nat} →
                (hdeg :
                    @Eq.{1} Nat
                      (@Module.finrank.{u_1, u_2} F K
                        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_1} F
                          (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst)))
                        (@Semiring.toAddCommMonoid.{u_2} K
                          (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                            (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1))))
                        (@Algebra.toModule.{u_1, u_2} F K
                          (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
                          (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                            (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))
                          inst_4))
                      (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 2) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 2)))) →
                  (v : Fin n → K) →
                    @Eq.{u_2 + 1} K (@Atlas.Unitary.hermitian.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n v v)
                        (@OfNat.ofNat.{u_2} K (nat_lit 0)
                          (@Zero.toOfNat0.{u_2} K
                            (@MulZeroClass.toZero.{u_2} K
                              (@instMulZeroClassOfSemiring.{u_2} K
                                (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                                  (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1))))))) →
                      @Subgroup.{u_2} (@Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
                        (@Atlas.Unitary.group_su.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg) :=
fun {F} {K} [inst : Field.{u_1} F] [inst_1 : Field.{u_2} K] [inst_2 : Finite.{u_1 + 1} F] [inst_3 : Finite.{u_2 + 1} K]
    [inst_4 :
      @Algebra.{u_1, u_2} F K (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
          (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))]
    {n} hdeg v hv =>
  @MonoidHom.range.{u_2, u_2}
    (Multiplicative.{u_2}
      ↥(@LinearMap.ker.{u_1, u_1, u_2, u_1} F F K F
          (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_1} F
            (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst)))

DEFINITION Atlas.Unitary.lineTransvectionHom SOURCE Atlas.LinearGroups.Unitary.LineTransvections
def Atlas.Unitary.lineTransvectionHom.{u_1, u_2} : {F : Type u_1} →
  {K : Type u_2} →
    [inst : Field.{u_1} F] →
      [inst_1 : Field.{u_2} K] →
        [inst_2 : Finite.{u_1 + 1} F] →
          [inst_3 : Finite.{u_2 + 1} K] →
            [inst_4 :
                @Algebra.{u_1, u_2} F K (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
                  (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                    (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))] →
              {n : Nat} →
                (hdeg :
                    @Eq.{1} Nat
                      (@Module.finrank.{u_1, u_2} F K
                        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_1} F
                          (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst)))

                          (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                            (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1))))

                          (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                            (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))
                          inst_4))
                      (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 2) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 2)))) →
                  (v : Fin n → K) →
                    @Eq.{u_2 + 1} K (@Atlas.Unitary.hermitian.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n v v)

DEFINITION Atlas.FiniteQuadratic.trace SOURCE Atlas.FieldTheory.FiniteQuadraticExtension
@[reducible] def Atlas.FiniteQuadratic.trace.{u_1, u_2} : (F : Type u_1) →
  (K : Type u_2) →
    [inst : Field.{u_1} F] →
      [inst_1 : Field.{u_2} K] →
        [inst_2 :
            @Algebra.{u_1, u_2} F K (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
              (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))] →
          @LinearMap.{u_1, u_1, u_2, u_1} F F
            (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_1} F
              (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst)))
            (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_1} F
              (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst)))
            (@RingHom.id.{u_1} F
              (@Semiring.toNonAssocSemiring.{u_1} F
                (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_1} F
                  (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst)))))
            K F
            (@Semiring.toAddCommMonoid.{u_2} K
              (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1))))
            (@Semiring.toAddCommMonoid.{u_1} F
              (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_1} F
                (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))))
            (@Algebra.toModule.{u_1, u_2} F K (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
              (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))
              inst_2)
            (@Semiring.toModule.{u_1} F
              (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_1} F
                (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst)))) :=
fun F K [inst : Field.{u_1} F] [inst_1 : Field.{u_2} K]
    [inst_2 :
      @Algebra.{u_1, u_2} F K (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
          (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))] =>
  @Algebra.trace.{u_1, u_2} F K (@Field.toCommRing.{u_1} F inst) (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1) inst_2
DEFINITION Atlas.Unitary.transvection SOURCE Atlas.LinearGroups.Unitary.Transvections
def Atlas.Unitary.transvection.{u_1, u_2} : {F : Type u_1} →
  {K : Type u_2} →
    [inst : Field.{u_1} F] →
      [inst_1 : Field.{u_2} K] →
        [inst_2 : Finite.{u_1 + 1} F] →
          [inst_3 : Finite.{u_2 + 1} K] →
            [inst_4 :
                @Algebra.{u_1, u_2} F K (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
                  (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                    (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))] →
              {n : Nat} →
                (hdeg :
                    @Eq.{1} Nat
                      (@Module.finrank.{u_1, u_2} F K
                        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_1} F
                          (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst)))
                        (@Semiring.toAddCommMonoid.{u_2} K
                          (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                            (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1))))
                        (@Algebra.toModule.{u_1, u_2} F K
                          (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
                          (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                            (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))
                          inst_4))
                      (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 2) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 2)))) →
                  (v : Fin n → K) →
                    (a : K) →
                      @Eq.{u_2 + 1} K (@Atlas.Unitary.hermitian.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n v v)
                          (@OfNat.ofNat.{u_2} K (nat_lit 0)
                            (@Zero.toOfNat0.{u_2} K
                              (@MulZeroClass.toZero.{u_2} K
                                (@instMulZeroClassOfSemiring.{u_2} K
                                  (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                                    (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1))))))) →
                        @Eq.{u_2 + 1} K
                            (@HAdd.hAdd.{u_2, u_2, u_2} K K K
                              (@instHAdd.{u_2} K
                                (@Distrib.toAdd.{u_2} K
                                  (@instDistribOfSemiring.{u_2} K
                                    (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                                      (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1))))))
                              a
                              (@DFunLike.coe.{u_2 + 1, u_2 + 1, u_2 + 1}
                                (@AlgEquiv.{u_1, u_2, u_2} F K K
                                  (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
                                  (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                                    (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))
                                  (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                                    (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))
                                  inst_4 inst_4)
                                K (fun x => K)
                                (@AlgEquiv.instFunLike.{u_1, u_2, u_2} F K K
                                  (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
                                  (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                                    (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))
                                  (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                                    (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))
                                  inst_4 inst_4)
                                (@Atlas.FiniteQuadratic.conjugation.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4) a))
                            (@OfNat.ofNat.{u_2} K (nat_lit 0)
                              (@Zero.toOfNat0.{u_2} K
                                (@MulZeroClass.toZero.{u_2} K
                                  (@instMulZeroClassOfSemiring.{u_2} K
                                    (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                                      (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1))))))) →
                          @Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg :=
fun {F} {K} [inst : Field.{u_1} F] [inst_1 : Field.{u_2} K] [inst_2 : Finite.{u_1 + 1} F] [inst_3 : Finite.{u_2 + 1} K]
    [inst_4 :
      @Algebra.{u_1, u_2} F K (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
          (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))]
    {n} hdeg v a hv ha =>
  @Subtype.mk.{u_2 + 1} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
    (fun x =>
      @Membership.mem.{u_2, u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
        (@Submonoid.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
          (@MulZeroOneClass.toMulOneClass.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
            (@instMulZeroOneClassOfSemiring.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
              (@Matrix.semiring.{u_2, 0} (Fin n) K
                (@CommSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K (@CommRing.toCommSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1)))
                (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n)))))
        (@SetLike.instMembership.{u_2, u_2}
          (@Submonoid.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
            (@MulZeroOneClass.toMulOneClass.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
              (@instMulZeroOneClassOfSemiring.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                (@Matrix.semiring.{u_2, 0} (Fin n) K
                  (@CommSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                    (@CommRing.toCommSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1)))
                  (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n)))))
          (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
          (@Submonoid.instSetLike.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
            (@MulZeroOneClass.toMulOneClass.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
              (@instMulZeroOneClassOfSemiring.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                (@Matrix.semiring.{u_2, 0} (Fin n) K
                  (@CommSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                    (@CommRing.toCommSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1)))
                  (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n))))))
        (@Matrix.specialUnitaryGroup.{0, u_2} (Fin n) (instDecidableEqFin n) (Fin.fintype n) K
          (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1)
          (@Atlas.Unitary.conjugationStarRing.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 hdeg))
        x)
    (@Atlas.Unitary.transvectionMatrix.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n v a) ⋯
DEFINITION Atlas.Unitary.transvectionMatrix SOURCE Atlas.LinearGroups.Unitary.Transvections
def Atlas.Unitary.transvectionMatrix.{u_1, u_2} : {F : Type u_1} →
  {K : Type u_2} →
    [inst : Field.{u_1} F] →
      [inst_1 : Field.{u_2} K] →
        [Finite.{u_1 + 1} F] →
          [Finite.{u_2 + 1} K] →
            [@Algebra.{u_1, u_2} F K (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
                  (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                    (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))] →
              {n : Nat} → (Fin n → K) → K → Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K :=
fun {F} {K} [inst : Field.{u_1} F] [inst_1 : Field.{u_2} K] [inst_2 : Finite.{u_1 + 1} F] [inst_3 : Finite.{u_2 + 1} K]
    [inst_4 :
      @Algebra.{u_1, u_2} F K (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
          (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))]
    {n} v a =>
  @HAdd.hAdd.{u_2, u_2, u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K) (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
    (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
    (@instHAdd.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
      (@Matrix.add.{u_2, 0, 0} (Fin n) (Fin n) K
        (@Distrib.toAdd.{u_2} K
          (@instDistribOfSemiring.{u_2} K
            (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
              (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))))))
    (@OfNat.ofNat.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K) (nat_lit 1)
      (@One.toOfNat1.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
        (@Matrix.one.{u_2, 0} (Fin n) K (instDecidableEqFin n)
          (@MulZeroClass.toZero.{u_2} K
            (@instMulZeroClassOfSemiring.{u_2} K
              (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))))
          (@AddMonoidWithOne.toOne.{u_2} K
            (@AddGroupWithOne.toAddMonoidWithOne.{u_2} K
              (@Ring.toAddGroupWithOne.{u_2} K
                (@DivisionRing.toRing.{u_2} K (@Field.toDivisionRing.{u_2} K inst_1))))))))
    (@Matrix.vecMulVec.{u_2, 0, 0} (Fin n) (Fin n) K
      (@Distrib.toMul.{u_2} K
        (@instDistribOfSemiring.{u_2} K
          (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
            (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))))
      v fun i =>
      @HMul.hMul.{u_2, u_2, u_2} K K K
        (@instHMul.{u_2} K
          (@Distrib.toMul.{u_2} K
            (@instDistribOfSemiring.{u_2} K
              (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1))))))
        a
        (@DFunLike.coe.{u_2 + 1, u_2 + 1, u_2 + 1}
          (@AlgEquiv.{u_1, u_2, u_2} F K K (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
            (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
              (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))
            (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
              (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))
            inst_4 inst_4)
          K (fun x => K)
          (@AlgEquiv.instFunLike.{u_1, u_2, u_2} F K K
            (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
            (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K

            inst_4 inst_4)

def Atlas.Unitary.hermitian.{u_1, u_2} : (F : Type u_1) →
  (K : Type u_2) →
    [inst : Field.{u_1} F] →
      [inst_1 : Field.{u_2} K] →
        [Finite.{u_1 + 1} F] →
          [Finite.{u_2 + 1} K] →
            [@Algebra.{u_1, u_2} F K (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
                  (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                    (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))] →
              {n : Nat} → (Fin n → K) → (Fin n → K) → K :=
fun F K [inst : Field.{u_1} F] [inst_1 : Field.{u_2} K] [inst_2 : Finite.{u_1 + 1} F] [inst_3 : Finite.{u_2 + 1} K]
    [inst_4 :
      @Algebra.{u_1, u_2} F K (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
          (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))]
    {n} v w =>
  ∑ i,
    @HMul.hMul.{u_2, u_2, u_2} K K K
      (@instHMul.{u_2} K
        (@Distrib.toMul.{u_2} K
          (@instDistribOfSemiring.{u_2} K
            (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
              (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1))))))
      (v i)
      (@DFunLike.coe.{u_2 + 1, u_2 + 1, u_2 + 1}
        (@AlgEquiv.{u_1, u_2, u_2} F K K (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
          (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
            (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))
          (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
            (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))
          inst_4 inst_4)
        K (fun x => K)
        (@AlgEquiv.instFunLike.{u_1, u_2, u_2} F K K
          (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
          (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
            (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))
          (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
            (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))
          inst_4 inst_4)
        (@Atlas.FiniteQuadratic.conjugation.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4) (w i))

    [inst : Field.{u_1} F] →
      [inst_1 : Field.{u_2} K] →

          [inst_3 : Finite.{u_2 + 1} K] →
            [inst_4 :
                @Algebra.{u_1, u_2} F K (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
                  (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                    (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))] →
              (hdeg :
                  @Eq.{1} Nat
                    (@Module.finrank.{u_1, u_2} F K

                        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                          (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1))))

                        (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
                        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                          (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))
                        inst_4))
                    (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 2) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 2)))) →
                (a b : K) →
                  (ha :
                      @Eq.{u_2 + 1} K
                        (@HMul.hMul.{u_2, u_2, u_2} K K K
                          (@instHMul.{u_2} K
                            (@Distrib.toMul.{u_2} K
                              (@instDistribOfSemiring.{u_2} K
                                (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                                  (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1))))))
                          a
                          (@DFunLike.coe.{u_2 + 1, u_2 + 1, u_2 + 1}
                            (@AlgEquiv.{u_1, u_2, u_2} F K K
                              (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
                              (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                                (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))
                              (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                                (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))
                              inst_4 inst_4)
                            K (fun x => K)
                            (@AlgEquiv.instFunLike.{u_1, u_2, u_2} F K K
                              (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
                              (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                                (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))
                              (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                                (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))
                              inst_4 inst_4)
                            (@Atlas.FiniteQuadratic.conjugation.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4) a))
                        (@Neg.neg.{u_2} K
                          (@NegZeroClass.toNeg.{u_2} K
                            (@SubNegZeroMonoid.toNegZeroClass.{u_2} K
                              (@SubtractionMonoid.toSubNegZeroMonoid.{u_2} K
                                (@SubtractionCommMonoid.toSubtractionMonoid.{u_2} K
                                  (@AddCommGroup.toDivisionAddCommMonoid.{u_2} K
                                    (@Ring.toAddCommGroup.{u_2} K
                                      (@DivisionRing.toRing.{u_2} K (@Field.toDivisionRing.{u_2} K inst_1))))))))
                          (@OfNat.ofNat.{u_2} K (nat_lit 1)
                            (@One.toOfNat1.{u_2} K
                              (@AddMonoidWithOne.toOne.{u_2} K

                                    (@DivisionRing.toRing.{u_2} K (@Field.toDivisionRing.{u_2} K inst_1))))))))) →
                    (hb :
                        @Eq.{u_2 + 1} K
                          (@HAdd.hAdd.{u_2, u_2, u_2} K K K
                            (@instHAdd.{u_2} K
                              (@Distrib.toAdd.{u_2} K
                                (@instDistribOfSemiring.{u_2} K

                            (@DFunLike.coe.{u_2 + 1, u_2 + 1, u_2 + 1}
                              (@AlgEquiv.{u_1, u_2, u_2} F K K
                                (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
                                (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K

                                (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
                                (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K

                          (@OfNat.ofNat.{u_2} K (nat_lit 1)
                            (@One.toOfNat1.{u_2} K
                              (@AddMonoidWithOne.toOne.{u_2} K
                                (@AddGroupWithOne.toAddMonoidWithOne.{u_2} K
                                  (@Ring.toAddGroupWithOne.{u_2} K
                                    (@DivisionRing.toRing.{u_2} K (@Field.toDivisionRing.{u_2} K inst_1)))))))) →
                      Equiv.{max 1 (u_2 + 1), u_2 + 1}
                        (@Subtype.{u_2 + 1} (Prod.{u_2, u_2} K K) fun p =>

DEFINITION Atlas.Unitary.rootThreeCondition SOURCE Atlas.LinearGroups.Unitary.GenerationThree
def Atlas.Unitary.rootThreeCondition.{u_1, u_2} : {F : Type u_1} →
  {K : Type u_2} →
    [inst : Field.{u_1} F] →
      [inst_1 : Field.{u_2} K] →
        [Finite.{u_1 + 1} F] →
          [Finite.{u_2 + 1} K] →
            [@Algebra.{u_1, u_2} F K (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
                  (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                    (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))] →
              K → K → Prop :=
fun {F} {K} [inst : Field.{u_1} F] [inst_1 : Field.{u_2} K] [inst_2 : Finite.{u_1 + 1} F] [inst_3 : Finite.{u_2 + 1} K]
    [inst_4 :
      @Algebra.{u_1, u_2} F K (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
          (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))]
    a b =>
  @Eq.{u_2 + 1} K
    (@HAdd.hAdd.{u_2, u_2, u_2} K K K
      (@instHAdd.{u_2} K
        (@Distrib.toAdd.{u_2} K
          (@instDistribOfSemiring.{u_2} K
            (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
              (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1))))))
      (@HAdd.hAdd.{u_2, u_2, u_2} K K K
        (@instHAdd.{u_2} K
          (@Distrib.toAdd.{u_2} K
            (@instDistribOfSemiring.{u_2} K
              (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1))))))
        (@HMul.hMul.{u_2, u_2, u_2} K K K
          (@instHMul.{u_2} K
            (@Distrib.toMul.{u_2} K

          a

            (@AlgEquiv.{u_1, u_2, u_2} F K K (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))

              inst_4 inst_4)
            K (fun x => K)
            (@AlgEquiv.instFunLike.{u_1, u_2, u_2} F K K
              (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))

              inst_4 inst_4)

        b)
      (@DFunLike.coe.{u_2 + 1, u_2 + 1, u_2 + 1}
        (@AlgEquiv.{u_1, u_2, u_2} F K K (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))

            (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))

            (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))

DEFINITION Atlas.Unitary.threeRootElement SOURCE Atlas.LinearGroups.Unitary.ThreeRootGroup
def Atlas.Unitary.threeRootElement.{u_1, u_2} : {F : Type u_1} →

                          (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))
                        inst_4))

                (a b : K) →

                              (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K

DEFINITION Atlas.Unitary.specialUnitaryFixedEquiv SOURCE Atlas.LinearGroups.Unitary.FixedPoints
def Atlas.Unitary.specialUnitaryFixedEquiv.{u_1, u_2} : {F : Type u_1} →
  {K : Type u_2} →
    [inst : Field.{u_1} F] →
      [inst_1 : Field.{u_2} K] →
        [inst_2 : Finite.{u_1 + 1} F] →
          [inst_3 : Finite.{u_2 + 1} K] →
            [inst_4 :
                @Algebra.{u_1, u_2} F K (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
                  (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                    (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))] →
              (hdeg :
                  @Eq.{1} Nat
                    (@Module.finrank.{u_1, u_2} F K
                      (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_1} F
                        (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst)))
                      (@Semiring.toAddCommMonoid.{u_2} K
                        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                          (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1))))
                      (@Algebra.toModule.{u_1, u_2} F K
                        (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
                        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                          (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))
                        inst_4))
                    (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 2) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 2)))) →
                (n : Nat) →
                  @MulEquiv.{u_2, u_2} (@Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
                    (↥(@Atlas.Unitary.graphFieldFixed.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 hdeg n))
                    (@Submonoid.mul.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                      (@MulZeroOneClass.toMulOneClass.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                        (@instMulZeroOneClassOfSemiring.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                          (@Matrix.semiring.{u_2, 0} (Fin n) K
                            (@CommSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                              (@CommRing.toCommSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1)))
                            (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n))))
                      (@Matrix.specialUnitaryGroup.{0, u_2} (Fin n) (instDecidableEqFin n) (Fin.fintype n) K
                        (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1)
                        (@Atlas.Unitary.conjugationStarRing.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 hdeg)))
                    (@Subgroup.mul.{u_2}
                      (@Matrix.SpecialLinearGroup.{0, u_2} (Fin n) (instDecidableEqFin n) (Fin.fintype n) K
                        (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1))
                      (@Matrix.SpecialLinearGroup.instGroup.{0, u_2} (Fin n) (instDecidableEqFin n) (Fin.fintype n) K
                        (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1))
                      (@Atlas.Unitary.graphFieldFixed.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 hdeg n)) :=
fun {F} {K} [inst : Field.{u_1} F] [inst_1 : Field.{u_2} K] [inst_2 : Finite.{u_1 + 1} F] [inst_3 : Finite.{u_2 + 1} K]
    [inst_4 :
      @Algebra.{u_1, u_2} F K (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
          (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))]
    hdeg n =>
  @MulEquiv.mk.{u_2, u_2} (@Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
    (↥(@Atlas.Unitary.graphFieldFixed.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 hdeg n))

              @Eq.{1} Nat

                                              (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_4} L
                                                (@Field.toSemifield.{u_4} L inst_3))))
                                          (@Algebra.toModule.{u_2, u_4} F' L
                                            (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_2} F' (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} F' inst_1))
                                            (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_4} L
                                              (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_4} L
                                                (@Field.toSemifield.{u_4} L inst_3)))
                                            inst_9))
                                        (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 2) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 2)))) →
                                    (n : Nat) →
                                      @MulEquiv.{u_3, u_4}
                                        (@Atlas.Unitary.PSU.{u_1, u_3} F K inst inst_2 inst_4 inst_6 inst_8 n hK)
                                        (@Atlas.Unitary.PSU.{u_2, u_4} F' L inst_1 inst_3 inst_5 inst_7 inst_9 n hL)
                                        (@MulOne.toMul.{u_3}
                                          (@Atlas.Unitary.PSU.{u_1, u_3} F K inst inst_2 inst_4 inst_6 inst_8 n hK)
                                          (@MulOneClass.toMulOne.{u_3}
                                            (@Atlas.Unitary.PSU.{u_1, u_3} F K inst inst_2 inst_4 inst_6 inst_8 n hK)
                                            (@Monoid.toMulOneClass.{u_3}
                                              (@Atlas.Unitary.PSU.{u_1, u_3} F K inst inst_2 inst_4 inst_6 inst_8 n hK)
                                              (@DivInvMonoid.toMonoid.{u_3}
                                                (@Atlas.Unitary.PSU.{u_1, u_3} F K inst inst_2 inst_4 inst_6 inst_8 n
                                                  hK)
                                                (@Group.toDivInvMonoid.{u_3}
                                                  (@Atlas.Unitary.PSU.{u_1, u_3} F K inst inst_2 inst_4 inst_6 inst_8 n
                                                    hK)
                                                  (@QuotientGroup.Quotient.group.{u_3}
                                                    (@Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_1, u_3} F K inst inst_2 inst_4 inst_6 inst_8 n
                                                      hK)
                                                    (@Atlas.Unitary.group_su.{u_1, u_3} F K inst inst_2 inst_4 inst_6
                                                      inst_8 n hK)
                                                    (@Subgroup.center.{u_3}
                                                      (@Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_1, u_3} F K inst inst_2 inst_4 inst_6 inst_8
                                                        n hK)
                                                      (@Atlas.Unitary.group_su.{u_1, u_3} F K inst inst_2 inst_4 inst_6
                                                        inst_8 n hK))
                                                    ⋯))))))
                                        (@MulOne.toMul.{u_4}
                                          (@Atlas.Unitary.PSU.{u_2, u_4} F' L inst_1 inst_3 inst_5 inst_7 inst_9 n hL)
                                          (@MulOneClass.toMulOne.{u_4}
                                            (@Atlas.Unitary.PSU.{u_2, u_4} F' L inst_1 inst_3 inst_5 inst_7 inst_9 n hL)
                                            (@Monoid.toMulOneClass.{u_4}
                                              (@Atlas.Unitary.PSU.{u_2, u_4} F' L inst_1 inst_3 inst_5 inst_7 inst_9 n
                                                hL)
                                              (@DivInvMonoid.toMonoid.{u_4}
                                                (@Atlas.Unitary.PSU.{u_2, u_4} F' L inst_1 inst_3 inst_5 inst_7 inst_9 n
                                                  hL)
                                                (@Group.toDivInvMonoid.{u_4}
                                                  (@Atlas.Unitary.PSU.{u_2, u_4} F' L inst_1 inst_3 inst_5 inst_7 inst_9
                                                    n hL)
                                                  (@QuotientGroup.Quotient.group.{u_4}
                                                    (@Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_2, u_4} F' L inst_1 inst_3 inst_5 inst_7
                                                      inst_9 n hL)
                                                    (@Atlas.Unitary.group_su.{u_2, u_4} F' L inst_1 inst_3 inst_5 inst_7
                                                      inst_9 n hL)
                                                    (@Subgroup.center.{u_4}
                                                      (@Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_2, u_4} F' L inst_1 inst_3 inst_5 inst_7
                                                        inst_9 n hL)
                                                      (@Atlas.Unitary.group_su.{u_2, u_4} F' L inst_1 inst_3 inst_5
                                                        inst_7 inst_9 n hL))
                                                    ⋯)))))) :=
fun {F} {F'} {K} {L} [inst : Field.{u_1} F] [inst_1 : Field.{u_2} F'] [inst_2 : Field.{u_3} K] [inst_3 : Field.{u_4} L]
    [inst_4 : Finite.{u_1 + 1} F] [inst_5 : Finite.{u_2 + 1} F'] [inst_6 : Finite.{u_3 + 1} K]
    [inst_7 : Finite.{u_4 + 1} L]
    [inst_8 :
      @Algebra.{u_1, u_3} F K (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_3} K
          (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_3} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_3} K inst_2)))]
    [inst_9 :
      @Algebra.{u_2, u_4} F' L (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_2} F' (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} F' inst_1))
        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_4} L
          (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_4} L (@Field.toSemifield.{u_4} L inst_3)))]
    e hcard hK hL n =>
  @QuotientGroup.congr.{u_3, u_4} (@Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_1, u_3} F K inst inst_2 inst_4 inst_6 inst_8 n hK)
    (@Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_2, u_4} F' L inst_1 inst_3 inst_5 inst_7 inst_9 n hL)
    (@Atlas.Unitary.group_su.{u_1, u_3} F K inst inst_2 inst_4 inst_6 inst_8 n hK)
    (@Atlas.Unitary.group_su.{u_2, u_4} F' L inst_1 inst_3 inst_5 inst_7 inst_9 n hL)
    (@Subgroup.center.{u_3} (@Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_1, u_3} F K inst inst_2 inst_4 inst_6 inst_8 n hK)
      (@Atlas.Unitary.group_su.{u_1, u_3} F K inst inst_2 inst_4 inst_6 inst_8 n hK))
    (@Subgroup.center.{u_4} (@Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_2, u_4} F' L inst_1 inst_3 inst_5 inst_7 inst_9 n hL)
      (@Atlas.Unitary.group_su.{u_2, u_4} F' L inst_1 inst_3 inst_5 inst_7 inst_9 n hL))
    ⋯ ⋯
    (@Atlas.Unitary.specialUnitaryBaseChangeEquiv.{u_1, u_2, u_3, u_4} F F' K L inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 inst_5
      inst_6 inst_7 inst_8 inst_9 e hcard hK hL n)
    ⋯
DEFINITION Atlas.Unitary.specialUnitaryBaseChangeEquiv SOURCE Atlas.LinearGroups.Unitary.BaseFieldTransport
def Atlas.Unitary.specialUnitaryBaseChangeEquiv.{u_1, u_2, u_3, u_4} : {F : Type u_1} →
  {F' : Type u_2} →
    {K : Type u_3} →
      {L : Type u_4} →
        [inst : Field.{u_1} F] →
          [inst_1 : Field.{u_2} F'] →
            [inst_2 : Field.{u_3} K] →
              [inst_3 : Field.{u_4} L] →
                [inst_4 : Finite.{u_1 + 1} F] →
                  [inst_5 : Finite.{u_2 + 1} F'] →
                    [inst_6 : Finite.{u_3 + 1} K] →
                      [inst_7 : Finite.{u_4 + 1} L] →
                        [inst_8 :

                              (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
                              (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_3} K
                                (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_3} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_3} K inst_2)))] →
                          [inst_9 :
                              @Algebra.{u_2, u_4} F' L

                                    (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_4} L

                                  (@instDistribOfSemiring.{u_4} L
                                    (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_4} L
                                      (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_4} L (@Field.toSemifield.{u_4} L inst_3))))) →
                              @Eq.{1} Nat (Nat.card.{u_1} F) (Nat.card.{u_2} F') →
                                (hK :

                                      (@Module.finrank.{u_1, u_3} F K
                                        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_1} F
                                          (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst)))

                                          (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
                                          (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_3} K
                                            (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_3} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_3} K inst_2)))
                                          inst_8))

                                          (@Semiring.toAddCommMonoid.{u_4} L
                                            (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_4} L
                                              (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_4} L

                                          (@Algebra.toModule.{u_2, u_4} F' L

                                              (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_4} L

                                            inst_9))

                                            (Fin.fintype n) K (@Field.toCommRing.{u_3} K inst_2)
                                            (@Atlas.Unitary.conjugationStarRing.{u_1, u_3} F K inst inst_2 inst_4 inst_6

                                            (@instMulZeroOneClassOfSemiring.{u_4} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_4} (Fin n) (Fin n) L)

                                                  (@CommRing.toCommSemiring.{u_4} L (@Field.toCommRing.{u_4} L inst_3)))

                                              inst_7 inst_9 hL))) :=
fun {F} {F'} {K} {L} [inst : Field.{u_1} F] [inst_1 : Field.{u_2} F'] [inst_2 : Field.{u_3} K] [inst_3 : Field.{u_4} L]
    [inst_4 : Finite.{u_1 + 1} F] [inst_5 : Finite.{u_2 + 1} F'] [inst_6 : Finite.{u_3 + 1} K]
    [inst_7 : Finite.{u_4 + 1} L]
    [inst_8 :
      @Algebra.{u_1, u_3} F K (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_3} K
          (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_3} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_3} K inst_2)))]
    [inst_9 :
      @Algebra.{u_2, u_4} F' L (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_2} F' (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} F' inst_1))
        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_4} L
          (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_4} L (@Field.toSemifield.{u_4} L inst_3)))]
    e hcard hK hL n =>
  @MulEquiv.mk.{u_3, u_4} (@Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_1, u_3} F K inst inst_2 inst_4 inst_6 inst_8 n hK)
    (@Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_2, u_4} F' L inst_1 inst_3 inst_5 inst_7 inst_9 n hL)
    (@Submonoid.mul.{u_3} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_3} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
      (@MulZeroOneClass.toMulOneClass.{u_3} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_3} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
        (@instMulZeroOneClassOfSemiring.{u_3} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_3} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
          (@Matrix.semiring.{u_3, 0} (Fin n) K
            (@CommSemiring.toSemiring.{u_3} K (@CommRing.toCommSemiring.{u_3} K (@Field.toCommRing.{u_3} K inst_2)))
            (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n))))
      (@Matrix.specialUnitaryGroup.{0, u_3} (Fin n) (instDecidableEqFin n) (Fin.fintype n) K
        (@Field.toCommRing.{u_3} K inst_2)
        (@Atlas.Unitary.conjugationStarRing.{u_1, u_3} F K inst inst_2 inst_4 inst_6 inst_8 hK)))
    (@Submonoid.mul.{u_4} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_4} (Fin n) (Fin n) L)
      (@MulZeroOneClass.toMulOneClass.{u_4} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_4} (Fin n) (Fin n) L)
        (@instMulZeroOneClassOfSemiring.{u_4} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_4} (Fin n) (Fin n) L)
          (@Matrix.semiring.{u_4, 0} (Fin n) L
            (@CommSemiring.toSemiring.{u_4} L (@CommRing.toCommSemiring.{u_4} L (@Field.toCommRing.{u_4} L inst_3)))
            (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n))))
      (@Matrix.specialUnitaryGroup.{0, u_4} (Fin n) (instDecidableEqFin n) (Fin.fintype n) L
        (@Field.toCommRing.{u_4} L inst_3)
        (@Atlas.Unitary.conjugationStarRing.{u_2, u_4} F' L inst_1 inst_3 inst_5 inst_7 inst_9 hL)))
    (@Equiv.mk.{u_3 + 1, u_4 + 1} (@Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_1, u_3} F K inst inst_2 inst_4 inst_6 inst_8 n hK)
      (@Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_2, u_4} F' L inst_1 inst_3 inst_5 inst_7 inst_9 n hL)
      (fun g =>
        @Subtype.mk.{u_4 + 1} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_4} (Fin n) (Fin n) L)
          (fun x =>
            @Membership.mem.{u_4, u_4} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_4} (Fin n) (Fin n) L)
              (@Submonoid.{u_4} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_4} (Fin n) (Fin n) L)
                (@MulZeroOneClass.toMulOneClass.{u_4} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_4} (Fin n) (Fin n) L)
                  (@instMulZeroOneClassOfSemiring.{u_4} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_4} (Fin n) (Fin n) L)
                    (@Matrix.semiring.{u_4, 0} (Fin n) L
                      (@CommSemiring.toSemiring.{u_4} L
                        (@CommRing.toCommSemiring.{u_4} L (@Field.toCommRing.{u_4} L inst_3)))
                      (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n)))))
              (@SetLike.instMembership.{u_4, u_4}
                (@Submonoid.{u_4} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_4} (Fin n) (Fin n) L)
                  (@MulZeroOneClass.toMulOneClass.{u_4} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_4} (Fin n) (Fin n) L)
                    (@instMulZeroOneClassOfSemiring.{u_4} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_4} (Fin n) (Fin n) L)
                      (@Matrix.semiring.{u_4, 0} (Fin n) L
                        (@CommSemiring.toSemiring.{u_4} L
                          (@CommRing.toCommSemiring.{u_4} L (@Field.toCommRing.{u_4} L inst_3)))
                        (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n)))))
                (Matrix.{0, 0, u_4} (Fin n) (Fin n) L)
                (@Submonoid.instSetLike.{u_4} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_4} (Fin n) (Fin n) L)
                  (@MulZeroOneClass.toMulOneClass.{u_4} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_4} (Fin n) (Fin n) L)
                    (@instMulZeroOneClassOfSemiring.{u_4} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_4} (Fin n) (Fin n) L)

                        (@CommSemiring.toSemiring.{u_4} L
                          (@CommRing.toCommSemiring.{u_4} L (@Field.toCommRing.{u_4} L inst_3)))
                        (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n))))))
              (@Matrix.specialUnitaryGroup.{0, u_4} (Fin n) (instDecidableEqFin n) (Fin.fintype n) L
                (@Field.toCommRing.{u_4} L inst_3)
                (@Atlas.Unitary.conjugationStarRing.{u_2, u_4} F' L inst_1 inst_3 inst_5 inst_7 inst_9 hL))
              x)
          (@Matrix.map.{u_3, u_4, 0, 0} (Fin n) (Fin n) K L
            (@Subtype.val.{u_3 + 1} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_3} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
              (fun x =>
                @Membership.mem.{u_3, u_3} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_3} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                  (@Submonoid.{u_3} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_3} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                    (@MulZeroOneClass.toMulOneClass.{u_3} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_3} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                      (@instMulZeroOneClassOfSemiring.{u_3} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_3} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                        (@Matrix.semiring.{u_3, 0} (Fin n) K
                          (@CommSemiring.toSemiring.{u_3} K
                            (@CommRing.toCommSemiring.{u_3} K (@Field.toCommRing.{u_3} K inst_2)))
                          (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n)))))
                  (@SetLike.instMembership.{u_3, u_3}
                    (@Submonoid.{u_3} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_3} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                      (@MulZeroOneClass.toMulOneClass.{u_3} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_3} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                        (@instMulZeroOneClassOfSemiring.{u_3} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_3} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                          (@Matrix.semiring.{u_3, 0} (Fin n) K
                            (@CommSemiring.toSemiring.{u_3} K
                              (@CommRing.toCommSemiring.{u_3} K (@Field.toCommRing.{u_3} K inst_2)))
                            (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n)))))
                    (Matrix.{0, 0, u_3} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                    (@Submonoid.instSetLike.{u_3} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_3} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                      (@MulZeroOneClass.toMulOneClass.{u_3} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_3} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                        (@instMulZeroOneClassOfSemiring.{u_3} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_3} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                          (@Matrix.semiring.{u_3, 0} (Fin n) K
                            (@CommSemiring.toSemiring.{u_3} K
                              (@CommRing.toCommSemiring.{u_3} K (@Field.toCommRing.{u_3} K inst_2)))
                            (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n))))))
                  (@Matrix.specialUnitaryGroup.{0, u_3} (Fin n) (instDecidableEqFin n) (Fin.fintype n) K
                    (@Field.toCommRing.{u_3} K inst_2)
                    (@Atlas.Unitary.conjugationStarRing.{u_1, u_3} F K inst inst_2 inst_4 inst_6 inst_8 hK))
                  x)
              g)
            (@DFunLike.coe.{max (u_3 + 1) (u_4 + 1), u_3 + 1, u_4 + 1}
              (@RingEquiv.{u_3, u_4} K L
                (@Distrib.toMul.{u_3} K
                  (@instDistribOfSemiring.{u_3} K
                    (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_3} K
                      (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_3} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_3} K inst_2)))))
                (@Distrib.toMul.{u_4} L
                  (@instDistribOfSemiring.{u_4} L
                    (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_4} L
                      (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_4} L (@Field.toSemifield.{u_4} L inst_3)))))
                (@Distrib.toAdd.{u_3} K
                  (@instDistribOfSemiring.{u_3} K
                    (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_3} K
                      (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_3} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_3} K inst_2)))))
                (@Distrib.toAdd.{u_4} L
                  (@instDistribOfSemiring.{u_4} L
                    (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_4} L
                      (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_4} L (@Field.toSemifield.{u_4} L inst_3))))))

              (@EquivLike.toFunLike.{max (u_3 + 1) (u_4 + 1), u_3 + 1, u_4 + 1}

                K L
                (@RingEquiv.instEquivLike.{u_3, u_4} K L
                  (@Distrib.toMul.{u_3} K
                    (@instDistribOfSemiring.{u_3} K
                      (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_3} K
                        (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_3} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_3} K inst_2)))))

              e))
          ⋯)
      (fun g =>
        @Subtype.mk.{u_3 + 1} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_3} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
          (fun x =>
            @Membership.mem.{u_3, u_3} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_3} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)

                (@MulZeroOneClass.toMulOneClass.{u_3} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_3} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)

                      (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n)))))
              (@SetLike.instMembership.{u_3, u_3}
                (@Submonoid.{u_3} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_3} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)

                          (@CommRing.toCommSemiring.{u_3} K (@Field.toCommRing.{u_3} K inst_2)))

                    (@instMulZeroOneClassOfSemiring.{u_3} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_3} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)

                          (@CommRing.toCommSemiring.{u_3} K (@Field.toCommRing.{u_3} K inst_2)))
                        (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n))))))

                (@Atlas.Unitary.conjugationStarRing.{u_1, u_3} F K inst inst_2 inst_4 inst_6 inst_8 hK))
              x)
          (@Matrix.map.{u_4, u_3, 0, 0} (Fin n) (Fin n) L K
            (@Subtype.val.{u_4 + 1} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_4} (Fin n) (Fin n) L)

                @Membership.mem.{u_4, u_4} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_4} (Fin n) (Fin n) L)

                    (@Atlas.Unitary.conjugationStarRing.{u_2, u_4} F' L inst_1 inst_3 inst_5 inst_7 inst_9 hL))
                  x)
              g)
            (@DFunLike.coe.{max (u_3 + 1) (u_4 + 1), u_4 + 1, u_3 + 1}
              (@RingEquiv.{u_4, u_3} L K
                (@Distrib.toMul.{u_4} L
                  (@instDistribOfSemiring.{u_4} L
                    (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_4} L
                      (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_4} L (@Field.toSemifield.{u_4} L inst_3)))))
                (@Distrib.toMul.{u_3} K
                  (@instDistribOfSemiring.{u_3} K
                    (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_3} K
                      (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_3} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_3} K inst_2)))))
                (@Distrib.toAdd.{u_4} L
                  (@instDistribOfSemiring.{u_4} L
                    (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_4} L
                      (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_4} L (@Field.toSemifield.{u_4} L inst_3)))))

                L K

                  (@Distrib.toMul.{u_4} L
