DEFINITION Atlas.Unitary.projectiveEmbedding SOURCE Atlas.LinearGroups.Unitary.Projective
def Atlas.Unitary.projectiveEmbedding.{u_1, u_2} : {F : Type u_1} →
  {K : Type u_2} →
    [inst : Field.{u_1} F] →
      [inst_1 : Field.{u_2} K] →
        [inst_2 : Finite.{u_1 + 1} F] →
          [inst_3 : Finite.{u_2 + 1} K] →
            [inst_4 :
                @Algebra.{u_1, u_2} F K (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
                  (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                    (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))] →
              {n : Nat} →
                (hdeg :
                    @Eq.{1} Nat
                      (@Module.finrank.{u_1, u_2} F K
                        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_1} F
                          (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst)))
                        (@Semiring.toAddCommMonoid.{u_2} K
                          (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                            (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1))))
                        (@Algebra.toModule.{u_1, u_2} F K
                          (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
                          (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                            (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))
                          inst_4))
                      (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 2) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 2)))) →
                  @LT.lt.{0} Nat instLTNat (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 0) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 0))) n →
                    @MonoidHom.{u_2, u_2} (@Atlas.Unitary.PSU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
                      (@Matrix.ProjGenLinGroup.{0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n) K
                        (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1))
                      (@MulOneClass.toMulOne.{u_2}
                        (@Atlas.Unitary.PSU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
                        (@Monoid.toMulOneClass.{u_2}
                          (@Atlas.Unitary.PSU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
                          (@DivInvMonoid.toMonoid.{u_2}
                            (@Atlas.Unitary.PSU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
                            (@Group.toDivInvMonoid.{u_2}
                              (@Atlas.Unitary.PSU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
                              (@QuotientGroup.Quotient.group.{u_2}
                                (@Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
                                (@Atlas.Unitary.group_su.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
                                (@Subgroup.center.{u_2}
                                  (@Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
                                  (@Atlas.Unitary.group_su.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg))
                                ⋯)))))
                      (@MulOneClass.toMulOne.{u_2}
                        (@Matrix.ProjGenLinGroup.{0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n) K
                          (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1))
                        (@Monoid.toMulOneClass.{u_2}
                          (@Matrix.ProjGenLinGroup.{0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n) K
                            (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1))
                          (@DivInvMonoid.toMonoid.{u_2}
                            (@Matrix.ProjGenLinGroup.{0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n) K
                              (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1))
                            (@Group.toDivInvMonoid.{u_2}
                              (@Matrix.ProjGenLinGroup.{0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n) K
                                (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1))
                              (@Matrix.instGroupProjGenLinGroup.{0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n)
                                K (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1)))))) :=
fun {F} {K} [inst : Field.{u_1} F] [inst_1 : Field.{u_2} K] [inst_2 : Finite.{u_1 + 1} F] [inst_3 : Finite.{u_2 + 1} K]
    [inst_4 :
      @Algebra.{u_1, u_2} F K (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
          (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))]
    {n} hdeg hn =>
  @QuotientGroup.lift.{u_2, u_2} (@Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
    (@Matrix.ProjGenLinGroup.{0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n) K
      (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1))
    (@Atlas.Unitary.group_su.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
    (@DivInvMonoid.toMonoid.{u_2}
      (@Matrix.ProjGenLinGroup.{0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n) K
        (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1))
      (@Group.toDivInvMonoid.{u_2}
        (@Matrix.ProjGenLinGroup.{0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n) K
          (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1))
        (@Matrix.instGroupProjGenLinGroup.{0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n) K
          (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1))))
    (@Subgroup.center.{u_2} (@Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
      (@Atlas.Unitary.group_su.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg))
    ⋯ (@Atlas.Unitary.specialToPGL.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg) ⋯
DEFINITION Atlas.Unitary.specialToPGL SOURCE Atlas.LinearGroups.Unitary.Projective
def Atlas.Unitary.specialToPGL.{u_1, u_2} : {F : Type u_1} →
  {K : Type u_2} →
    [inst : Field.{u_1} F] →
      [inst_1 : Field.{u_2} K] →
        [inst_2 : Finite.{u_1 + 1} F] →
          [inst_3 : Finite.{u_2 + 1} K] →
            [inst_4 :
                @Algebra.{u_1, u_2} F K (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
                  (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                    (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))] →
              {n : Nat} →
                (hdeg :
                    @Eq.{1} Nat
                      (@Module.finrank.{u_1, u_2} F K
                        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_1} F
                          (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst)))
                        (@Semiring.toAddCommMonoid.{u_2} K
                          (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                            (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1))))
                        (@Algebra.toModule.{u_1, u_2} F K
                          (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
                          (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                            (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))
                          inst_4))
                      (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 2) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 2)))) →
                  @MonoidHom.{u_2, u_2} (@Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
                    (@Matrix.ProjGenLinGroup.{0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n) K
                      (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1))
                    (@MulOneClass.toMulOne.{u_2}
                      (@Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
                      (@Submonoid.toMulOneClass.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                        (@MulZeroOneClass.toMulOneClass.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                          (@instMulZeroOneClassOfSemiring.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                            (@Matrix.semiring.{u_2, 0} (Fin n) K
                              (@CommSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                                (@CommRing.toCommSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1)))
                              (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n))))
                        (@Matrix.specialUnitaryGroup.{0, u_2} (Fin n) (instDecidableEqFin n) (Fin.fintype n) K
                          (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1)
                          (@Atlas.Unitary.conjugationStarRing.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 hdeg))))
                    (@MulOneClass.toMulOne.{u_2}
                      (@Matrix.ProjGenLinGroup.{0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n) K
                        (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1))
                      (@Monoid.toMulOneClass.{u_2}
                        (@Matrix.ProjGenLinGroup.{0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n) K
                          (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1))
                        (@DivInvMonoid.toMonoid.{u_2}
                          (@Matrix.ProjGenLinGroup.{0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n) K
                            (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1))
                          (@Group.toDivInvMonoid.{u_2}
                            (@Matrix.ProjGenLinGroup.{0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n) K
                              (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1))
                            (@Matrix.instGroupProjGenLinGroup.{0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n) K
                              (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1)))))) :=
fun {F} {K} [inst : Field.{u_1} F] [inst_1 : Field.{u_2} K] [inst_2 : Finite.{u_1 + 1} F] [inst_3 : Finite.{u_2 + 1} K]
    [inst_4 :
      @Algebra.{u_1, u_2} F K (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
          (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))]
    {n} hdeg =>
  @MonoidHom.comp.{u_2, u_2, u_2} (@Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
    (@Matrix.GeneralLinearGroup.{0, u_2} (Fin n) K (instDecidableEqFin n) (Fin.fintype n)
      (@CommSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K (@CommRing.toCommSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1))))
    (@Matrix.ProjGenLinGroup.{0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n) K
      (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1))
    (@MulOneClass.toMulOne.{u_2} (@Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
      (@Submonoid.toMulOneClass.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
        (@MulZeroOneClass.toMulOneClass.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
          (@instMulZeroOneClassOfSemiring.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
            (@Matrix.semiring.{u_2, 0} (Fin n) K
              (@CommSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K (@CommRing.toCommSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1)))
              (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n))))
        (@Matrix.specialUnitaryGroup.{0, u_2} (Fin n) (instDecidableEqFin n) (Fin.fintype n) K
          (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1)
          (@Atlas.Unitary.conjugationStarRing.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 hdeg))))
    (@MulOneClass.toMulOne.{u_2}
      (@Matrix.GeneralLinearGroup.{0, u_2} (Fin n) K (instDecidableEqFin n) (Fin.fintype n)
        (@CommSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K (@CommRing.toCommSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1))))
      (@Units.instMulOneClass.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
        (@Semiring.toMonoid.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
          (@Matrix.semiring.{u_2, 0} (Fin n) K
            (@CommSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K (@CommRing.toCommSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1)))
            (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n)))))
    (@MulOneClass.toMulOne.{u_2}
      (@Matrix.ProjGenLinGroup.{0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n) K
        (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1))
      (@Monoid.toMulOneClass.{u_2}
        (@Matrix.ProjGenLinGroup.{0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n) K
          (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1))
        (@DivInvMonoid.toMonoid.{u_2}
          (@Matrix.ProjGenLinGroup.{0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n) K
            (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1))
          (@Group.toDivInvMonoid.{u_2}
            (@Matrix.ProjGenLinGroup.{0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n) K
              (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1))
            (@Matrix.instGroupProjGenLinGroup.{0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n) K
              (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1))))))
    (@Matrix.ProjGenLinGroup.mk.{0, u_2} (Fin n) K (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n)
      (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1))
    (@Atlas.Unitary.specialToGL.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
DEFINITION Atlas.Unitary.specialToGL SOURCE Atlas.LinearGroups.Unitary.Projective
def Atlas.Unitary.specialToGL.{u_1, u_2} : {F : Type u_1} →
  {K : Type u_2} →
    [inst : Field.{u_1} F] →
      [inst_1 : Field.{u_2} K] →
        [inst_2 : Finite.{u_1 + 1} F] →
          [inst_3 : Finite.{u_2 + 1} K] →
            [inst_4 :
                @Algebra.{u_1, u_2} F K (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
                  (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                    (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))] →
              {n : Nat} →
                (hdeg :
                    @Eq.{1} Nat
                      (@Module.finrank.{u_1, u_2} F K
                        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_1} F
                          (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst)))
                        (@Semiring.toAddCommMonoid.{u_2} K
                          (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                            (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1))))
                        (@Algebra.toModule.{u_1, u_2} F K
                          (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
                          (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                            (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))
                          inst_4))
                      (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 2) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 2)))) →
                  @MonoidHom.{u_2, u_2} (@Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
                    (@Matrix.GeneralLinearGroup.{0, u_2} (Fin n) K (instDecidableEqFin n) (Fin.fintype n)
                      (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                        (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1))))
                    (@MulOneClass.toMulOne.{u_2}
                      (@Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
                      (@Submonoid.toMulOneClass.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                        (@MulZeroOneClass.toMulOneClass.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                          (@instMulZeroOneClassOfSemiring.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                            (@Matrix.semiring.{u_2, 0} (Fin n) K
                              (@CommSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                                (@CommRing.toCommSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1)))
                              (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n))))
                        (@Matrix.specialUnitaryGroup.{0, u_2} (Fin n) (instDecidableEqFin n) (Fin.fintype n) K
                          (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1)
                          (@Atlas.Unitary.conjugationStarRing.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 hdeg))))
                    (@MulOneClass.toMulOne.{u_2}
                      (@Matrix.GeneralLinearGroup.{0, u_2} (Fin n) K (instDecidableEqFin n) (Fin.fintype n)
                        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                          (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1))))
                      (@Units.instMulOneClass.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                        (@Semiring.toMonoid.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                          (@Matrix.semiring.{u_2, 0} (Fin n) K
                            (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                              (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))
                            (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n))))) :=
fun {F} {K} [inst : Field.{u_1} F] [inst_1 : Field.{u_2} K] [inst_2 : Finite.{u_1 + 1} F] [inst_3 : Finite.{u_2 + 1} K]
    [inst_4 :
      @Algebra.{u_1, u_2} F K (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
          (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))]
    {n} hdeg =>
  have this := @Atlas.Unitary.conjugationStarRing.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 hdeg;
  @MonoidHom.comp.{u_2, u_2, u_2} (@Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)

            (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n)))
        (@StarRing.toStarMul.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)

              (@Semiring.toNonUnitalSemiring.{u_2} K

                  (@CommRing.toCommSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1)))))
            (@Atlas.Unitary.conjugationStarRing.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 hdeg)))))
    (@Units.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
      (@Semiring.toMonoid.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
        (@Matrix.semiring.{u_2, 0} (Fin n) K
          (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
            (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))
          (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n))))
    (@MulOneClass.toMulOne.{u_2} (@Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
      (@Submonoid.toMulOneClass.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
        (@MulZeroOneClass.toMulOneClass.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
          (@instMulZeroOneClassOfSemiring.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)

              (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n))))
        (@Matrix.specialUnitaryGroup.{0, u_2} (Fin n) (instDecidableEqFin n) (Fin.fintype n) K
          (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1)
          (@Atlas.Unitary.conjugationStarRing.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 hdeg))))
    (@MulOneClass.toMulOne.{u_2}
      (↥(@unitary.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
          (@Semiring.toMonoid.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
            (@Matrix.semiring.{u_2, 0} (Fin n) K
              (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))
              (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n)))
          (@StarRing.toStarMul.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
            (@NonUnitalSemiring.toNonUnitalNonAssocSemiring.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)

                (@Semiring.toNonUnitalSemiring.{u_2} K

                (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))
              (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n))))
        (@unitary.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
          (@Semiring.toMonoid.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
            (@Matrix.semiring.{u_2, 0} (Fin n) K
              (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))
              (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n)))
          (@StarRing.toStarMul.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)

      (@Units.instMulOneClass.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)

DEFINITION Atlas.Unitary.specialInclusion SOURCE Atlas.LinearGroups.Unitary.Determinant
def Atlas.Unitary.specialInclusion.{u_1, u_2} : {F : Type u_1} →
  {K : Type u_2} →
    [inst : Field.{u_1} F] →
      [inst_1 : Field.{u_2} K] →
        [inst_2 : Finite.{u_1 + 1} F] →
          [inst_3 : Finite.{u_2 + 1} K] →
            [inst_4 :
                @Algebra.{u_1, u_2} F K (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
                  (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                    (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))] →
              {n : Nat} →
                (hdeg :
                    @Eq.{1} Nat
                      (@Module.finrank.{u_1, u_2} F K
                        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_1} F
                          (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst)))
                        (@Semiring.toAddCommMonoid.{u_2} K
                          (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                            (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1))))
                        (@Algebra.toModule.{u_1, u_2} F K
                          (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
                          (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                            (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))
                          inst_4))
                      (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 2) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 2)))) →
                  @MonoidHom.{u_2, u_2} (@Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
                    (@Atlas.Unitary.U.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
                    (@MulOneClass.toMulOne.{u_2}
                      (@Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
                      (@Submonoid.toMulOneClass.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                        (@MulZeroOneClass.toMulOneClass.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                          (@instMulZeroOneClassOfSemiring.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                            (@Matrix.semiring.{u_2, 0} (Fin n) K
                              (@CommSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                                (@CommRing.toCommSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1)))
                              (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n))))
                        (@Matrix.specialUnitaryGroup.{0, u_2} (Fin n) (instDecidableEqFin n) (Fin.fintype n) K
                          (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1)
                          (@Atlas.Unitary.conjugationStarRing.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 hdeg))))
                    (@MulOneClass.toMulOne.{u_2}
                      (@Atlas.Unitary.U.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
                      (@Submonoid.toMulOneClass.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                        (@MulZeroOneClass.toMulOneClass.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                          (@instMulZeroOneClassOfSemiring.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                            (@Matrix.semiring.{u_2, 0} (Fin n) K
                              (@CommSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                                (@CommRing.toCommSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1)))
                              (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n))))
                        (@Matrix.unitaryGroup.{0, u_2} (Fin n) (instDecidableEqFin n) (Fin.fintype n) K
                          (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1)
                          (@Atlas.Unitary.conjugationStarRing.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4
                            hdeg)))) :=
fun {F} {K} [inst : Field.{u_1} F] [inst_1 : Field.{u_2} K] [inst_2 : Finite.{u_1 + 1} F] [inst_3 : Finite.{u_2 + 1} K]
    [inst_4 :
      @Algebra.{u_1, u_2} F K (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
          (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))]
    {n} hdeg =>
  @MonoidHom.mk.{u_2, u_2} (@Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
    (@Atlas.Unitary.U.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
    (@MulOneClass.toMulOne.{u_2} (@Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
      (@Submonoid.toMulOneClass.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
        (@MulZeroOneClass.toMulOneClass.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
          (@instMulZeroOneClassOfSemiring.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
            (@Matrix.semiring.{u_2, 0} (Fin n) K
              (@CommSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K (@CommRing.toCommSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1)))
              (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n))))
        (@Matrix.specialUnitaryGroup.{0, u_2} (Fin n) (instDecidableEqFin n) (Fin.fintype n) K
          (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1)
          (@Atlas.Unitary.conjugationStarRing.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 hdeg))))
    (@MulOneClass.toMulOne.{u_2} (@Atlas.Unitary.U.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
      (@Submonoid.toMulOneClass.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
        (@MulZeroOneClass.toMulOneClass.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
          (@instMulZeroOneClassOfSemiring.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
            (@Matrix.semiring.{u_2, 0} (Fin n) K
              (@CommSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K (@CommRing.toCommSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1)))
              (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n))))
        (@Matrix.unitaryGroup.{0, u_2} (Fin n) (instDecidableEqFin n) (Fin.fintype n) K
          (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1)
          (@Atlas.Unitary.conjugationStarRing.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 hdeg))))
    (@OneHom.mk.{u_2, u_2} (@Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
      (@Atlas.Unitary.U.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
      (@MulOne.toOne.{u_2} (@Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
        (@MulOneClass.toMulOne.{u_2} (@Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
          (@Submonoid.toMulOneClass.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
            (@MulZeroOneClass.toMulOneClass.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
              (@instMulZeroOneClassOfSemiring.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                (@Matrix.semiring.{u_2, 0} (Fin n) K
                  (@CommSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                    (@CommRing.toCommSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1)))
                  (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n))))
            (@Matrix.specialUnitaryGroup.{0, u_2} (Fin n) (instDecidableEqFin n) (Fin.fintype n) K
              (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1)
              (@Atlas.Unitary.conjugationStarRing.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 hdeg)))))
      (@MulOne.toOne.{u_2} (@Atlas.Unitary.U.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
        (@MulOneClass.toMulOne.{u_2} (@Atlas.Unitary.U.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
          (@Submonoid.toMulOneClass.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
            (@MulZeroOneClass.toMulOneClass.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
              (@instMulZeroOneClassOfSemiring.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                (@Matrix.semiring.{u_2, 0} (Fin n) K
                  (@CommSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                    (@CommRing.toCommSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1)))
                  (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n))))
            (@Matrix.unitaryGroup.{0, u_2} (Fin n) (instDecidableEqFin n) (Fin.fintype n) K
              (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1)
              (@Atlas.Unitary.conjugationStarRing.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 hdeg)))))
      (fun g =>
        @Subtype.mk.{u_2 + 1} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
          (fun x =>
            @Membership.mem.{u_2, u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
              (@Submonoid.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                (@MulZeroOneClass.toMulOneClass.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                  (@instMulZeroOneClassOfSemiring.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                    (@Matrix.semiring.{u_2, 0} (Fin n) K
                      (@CommSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                        (@CommRing.toCommSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1)))
                      (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n)))))
              (@SetLike.instMembership.{u_2, u_2}
                (@Submonoid.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                  (@MulZeroOneClass.toMulOneClass.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                    (@instMulZeroOneClassOfSemiring.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                      (@Matrix.semiring.{u_2, 0} (Fin n) K
                        (@CommSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                          (@CommRing.toCommSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1)))
                        (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n)))))
                (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                (@Submonoid.instSetLike.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                  (@MulZeroOneClass.toMulOneClass.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                    (@instMulZeroOneClassOfSemiring.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                      (@Matrix.semiring.{u_2, 0} (Fin n) K
                        (@CommSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                          (@CommRing.toCommSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1)))
                        (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n))))))
              (@Matrix.unitaryGroup.{0, u_2} (Fin n) (instDecidableEqFin n) (Fin.fintype n) K
                (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1)
                (@Atlas.Unitary.conjugationStarRing.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 hdeg))
              x)
          (@Subtype.val.{u_2 + 1} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
            (fun x =>
              @Membership.mem.{u_2, u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                (@Submonoid.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                  (@MulZeroOneClass.toMulOneClass.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                    (@instMulZeroOneClassOfSemiring.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                      (@Matrix.semiring.{u_2, 0} (Fin n) K
                        (@CommSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                          (@CommRing.toCommSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1)))
                        (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n)))))
                (@SetLike.instMembership.{u_2, u_2}
                  (@Submonoid.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                    (@MulZeroOneClass.toMulOneClass.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                      (@instMulZeroOneClassOfSemiring.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                        (@Matrix.semiring.{u_2, 0} (Fin n) K
                          (@CommSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                            (@CommRing.toCommSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1)))
                          (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n)))))
                  (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                  (@Submonoid.instSetLike.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                    (@MulZeroOneClass.toMulOneClass.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                      (@instMulZeroOneClassOfSemiring.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                        (@Matrix.semiring.{u_2, 0} (Fin n) K
                          (@CommSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                            (@CommRing.toCommSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1)))
                          (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n))))))
                (@Matrix.specialUnitaryGroup.{0, u_2} (Fin n) (instDecidableEqFin n) (Fin.fintype n) K
                  (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1)
                  (@Atlas.Unitary.conjugationStarRing.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 hdeg))
                x)
            g)
          ⋯)
      ⋯)
    ⋯
DEFINITION Atlas.Unitary.U SOURCE Atlas.LinearGroups.Unitary.Basic
@[reducible] def Atlas.Unitary.U.{u_1, u_2} : (F : Type u_1) →
  (K : Type u_2) →
    [inst : Field.{u_1} F] →
      [inst_1 : Field.{u_2} K] →
        [Finite.{u_1 + 1} F] →
          [Finite.{u_2 + 1} K] →
            [inst_4 :
                @Algebra.{u_1, u_2} F K (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
                  (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                    (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))] →
              Nat →
                @Eq.{1} Nat
                    (@Module.finrank.{u_1, u_2} F K
                      (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_1} F
                        (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst)))
                      (@Semiring.toAddCommMonoid.{u_2} K
                        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                          (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1))))
                      (@Algebra.toModule.{u_1, u_2} F K
                        (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
                        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                          (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))
                        inst_4))
                    (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 2) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 2))) →
                  Type u_2 :=
fun F K [inst : Field.{u_1} F] [inst_1 : Field.{u_2} K] [inst_2 : Finite.{u_1 + 1} F] [inst_3 : Finite.{u_2 + 1} K]
    [inst_4 :
      @Algebra.{u_1, u_2} F K (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
          (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))]
    n hdeg =>
  ↥(@Matrix.unitaryGroup.{0, u_2} (Fin n) (instDecidableEqFin n) (Fin.fintype n) K (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1)
      (@Atlas.Unitary.conjugationStarRing.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 hdeg))
DEFINITION Atlas.Unitary.isotropicAction SOURCE Atlas.LinearGroups.Unitary.IsotropicAction
def Atlas.Unitary.isotropicAction.{u_1, u_2} : {F : Type u_1} →
  {K : Type u_2} →
    [inst : Field.{u_1} F] →
      [inst_1 : Field.{u_2} K] →
        [inst_2 : Finite.{u_1 + 1} F] →
          [inst_3 : Finite.{u_2 + 1} K] →
            [inst_4 :
                @Algebra.{u_1, u_2} F K (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
                  (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                    (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))] →
              {n : Nat} →
                (hdeg :
                    @Eq.{1} Nat
                      (@Module.finrank.{u_1, u_2} F K
                        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_1} F
                          (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst)))
                        (@Semiring.toAddCommMonoid.{u_2} K
                          (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                            (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1))))
                        (@Algebra.toModule.{u_1, u_2} F K
                          (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
                          (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                            (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))
                          inst_4))
                      (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 2) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 2)))) →
                  @LT.lt.{0} Nat instLTNat (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 0) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 0))) n →
                    @MulAction.{u_2, u_2} (@Atlas.Unitary.PSU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
                      (@Atlas.Unitary.IsotropicPoints.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n)
                      (@DivInvMonoid.toMonoid.{u_2}
                        (@Atlas.Unitary.PSU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
                        (@Group.toDivInvMonoid.{u_2}
                          (@Atlas.Unitary.PSU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
                          (@QuotientGroup.Quotient.group.{u_2}
                            (@Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
                            (@Atlas.Unitary.group_su.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
                            (@Subgroup.center.{u_2}
                              (@Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
                              (@Atlas.Unitary.group_su.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg))
                            ⋯))) :=
fun {F} {K} [inst : Field.{u_1} F] [inst_1 : Field.{u_2} K] [inst_2 : Finite.{u_1 + 1} F] [inst_3 : Finite.{u_2 + 1} K]
    [inst_4 :
      @Algebra.{u_1, u_2} F K (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
          (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))]
    {n} hdeg hn =>
  @MulAction.compHom.{u_2, u_2, u_2}
    (Equiv.Perm.{u_2 + 1} (@Atlas.Unitary.IsotropicPoints.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n))
    (@Atlas.Unitary.PSU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
    (@Atlas.Unitary.IsotropicPoints.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n)
    (@DivInvMonoid.toMonoid.{u_2}
      (Equiv.Perm.{u_2 + 1} (@Atlas.Unitary.IsotropicPoints.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n))
      (@Group.toDivInvMonoid.{u_2}
        (Equiv.Perm.{u_2 + 1} (@Atlas.Unitary.IsotropicPoints.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n))
        (@Equiv.Perm.permGroup.{u_2}
          (@Atlas.Unitary.IsotropicPoints.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n))))
    (Equiv.Perm.applyMulAction.{u_2} (@Atlas.Unitary.IsotropicPoints.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n))
    (@DivInvMonoid.toMonoid.{u_2} (@Atlas.Unitary.PSU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
      (@Group.toDivInvMonoid.{u_2} (@Atlas.Unitary.PSU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
        (@QuotientGroup.Quotient.group.{u_2} (@Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
          (@Atlas.Unitary.group_su.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
          (@Subgroup.center.{u_2} (@Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
            (@Atlas.Unitary.group_su.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg))
          ⋯)))
    (@Atlas.Unitary.projectiveIsotropicPerm.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg hn)
DEFINITION Atlas.Unitary.projectiveIsotropicPerm SOURCE Atlas.LinearGroups.Unitary.IsotropicAction
def Atlas.Unitary.projectiveIsotropicPerm.{u_1, u_2} : {F : Type u_1} →
  {K : Type u_2} →
    [inst : Field.{u_1} F] →
      [inst_1 : Field.{u_2} K] →
        [inst_2 : Finite.{u_1 + 1} F] →
          [inst_3 : Finite.{u_2 + 1} K] →
            [inst_4 :
                @Algebra.{u_1, u_2} F K (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
                  (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                    (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))] →
              {n : Nat} →
                (hdeg :
                    @Eq.{1} Nat
                      (@Module.finrank.{u_1, u_2} F K
                        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_1} F
                          (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst)))
                        (@Semiring.toAddCommMonoid.{u_2} K
                          (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                            (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1))))
                        (@Algebra.toModule.{u_1, u_2} F K
                          (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
                          (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                            (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))
                          inst_4))
                      (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 2) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 2)))) →
                  @LT.lt.{0} Nat instLTNat (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 0) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 0))) n →
                    @MonoidHom.{u_2, u_2} (@Atlas.Unitary.PSU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
                      (Equiv.Perm.{u_2 + 1}
                        (@Atlas.Unitary.IsotropicPoints.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n))
                      (@MulOneClass.toMulOne.{u_2}
                        (@Atlas.Unitary.PSU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
                        (@Monoid.toMulOneClass.{u_2}
                          (@Atlas.Unitary.PSU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
                          (@DivInvMonoid.toMonoid.{u_2}
                            (@Atlas.Unitary.PSU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
                            (@Group.toDivInvMonoid.{u_2}
                              (@Atlas.Unitary.PSU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
                              (@QuotientGroup.Quotient.group.{u_2}
                                (@Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
                                (@Atlas.Unitary.group_su.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
                                (@Subgroup.center.{u_2}
                                  (@Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
                                  (@Atlas.Unitary.group_su.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg))
                                ⋯)))))
                      (@MulOneClass.toMulOne.{u_2}
                        (Equiv.Perm.{u_2 + 1}
                          (@Atlas.Unitary.IsotropicPoints.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n))
                        (@Monoid.toMulOneClass.{u_2}
                          (Equiv.Perm.{u_2 + 1}
                            (@Atlas.Unitary.IsotropicPoints.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n))
                          (@DivInvMonoid.toMonoid.{u_2}
                            (Equiv.Perm.{u_2 + 1}
                              (@Atlas.Unitary.IsotropicPoints.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n))
                            (@Group.toDivInvMonoid.{u_2}
                              (Equiv.Perm.{u_2 + 1}
                                (@Atlas.Unitary.IsotropicPoints.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n))
                              (@Equiv.Perm.permGroup.{u_2}
                                (@Atlas.Unitary.IsotropicPoints.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4
                                  n)))))) :=
fun {F} {K} [inst : Field.{u_1} F] [inst_1 : Field.{u_2} K] [inst_2 : Finite.{u_1 + 1} F] [inst_3 : Finite.{u_2 + 1} K]
    [inst_4 :
      @Algebra.{u_1, u_2} F K (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
          (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))]
    {n} hdeg hn =>
  @QuotientGroup.lift.{u_2, u_2} (@Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
    (Equiv.Perm.{u_2 + 1} (@Atlas.Unitary.IsotropicPoints.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n))
    (@Atlas.Unitary.group_su.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
    (@DivInvMonoid.toMonoid.{u_2}
      (Equiv.Perm.{u_2 + 1} (@Atlas.Unitary.IsotropicPoints.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n))
      (@Group.toDivInvMonoid.{u_2}
        (Equiv.Perm.{u_2 + 1} (@Atlas.Unitary.IsotropicPoints.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n))
        (@Equiv.Perm.permGroup.{u_2}
          (@Atlas.Unitary.IsotropicPoints.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n))))
    (@Subgroup.center.{u_2} (@Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
      (@Atlas.Unitary.group_su.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg))
    ⋯ (@Atlas.Unitary.isotropicPerm.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg) ⋯
DEFINITION Atlas.Unitary.isotropicPerm SOURCE Atlas.LinearGroups.Unitary.IsotropicAction
def Atlas.Unitary.isotropicPerm.{u_1, u_2} : {F : Type u_1} →
  {K : Type u_2} →
    [inst : Field.{u_1} F] →
      [inst_1 : Field.{u_2} K] →
        [inst_2 : Finite.{u_1 + 1} F] →
          [inst_3 : Finite.{u_2 + 1} K] →
            [inst_4 :
                @Algebra.{u_1, u_2} F K (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
                  (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                    (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))] →
              {n : Nat} →
                (hdeg :
                    @Eq.{1} Nat
                      (@Module.finrank.{u_1, u_2} F K
                        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_1} F
                          (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst)))
                        (@Semiring.toAddCommMonoid.{u_2} K
                          (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                            (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1))))
                        (@Algebra.toModule.{u_1, u_2} F K
                          (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
                          (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                            (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))
                          inst_4))
                      (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 2) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 2)))) →
                  @MonoidHom.{u_2, u_2} (@Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
                    (Equiv.Perm.{u_2 + 1}
                      (@Atlas.Unitary.IsotropicPoints.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n))
                    (@MulOneClass.toMulOne.{u_2}
                      (@Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
                      (@Submonoid.toMulOneClass.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                        (@MulZeroOneClass.toMulOneClass.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                          (@instMulZeroOneClassOfSemiring.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                            (@Matrix.semiring.{u_2, 0} (Fin n) K
                              (@CommSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                                (@CommRing.toCommSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1)))
                              (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n))))
                        (@Matrix.specialUnitaryGroup.{0, u_2} (Fin n) (instDecidableEqFin n) (Fin.fintype n) K
                          (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1)
                          (@Atlas.Unitary.conjugationStarRing.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 hdeg))))
                    (@MulOneClass.toMulOne.{u_2}
                      (Equiv.Perm.{u_2 + 1}
                        (@Atlas.Unitary.IsotropicPoints.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n))
                      (@Monoid.toMulOneClass.{u_2}
                        (Equiv.Perm.{u_2 + 1}
                          (@Atlas.Unitary.IsotropicPoints.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n))
                        (@DivInvMonoid.toMonoid.{u_2}
                          (Equiv.Perm.{u_2 + 1}
                            (@Atlas.Unitary.IsotropicPoints.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n))
                          (@Group.toDivInvMonoid.{u_2}
                            (Equiv.Perm.{u_2 + 1}
                              (@Atlas.Unitary.IsotropicPoints.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n))
                            (@Equiv.Perm.permGroup.{u_2}
                              (@Atlas.Unitary.IsotropicPoints.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n)))))) :=
fun {F} {K} [inst : Field.{u_1} F] [inst_1 : Field.{u_2} K] [inst_2 : Finite.{u_1 + 1} F] [inst_3 : Finite.{u_2 + 1} K]
    [inst_4 :
      @Algebra.{u_1, u_2} F K (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
          (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))]
    {n} hdeg =>
  @MulAction.toPermHom.{u_2, u_2} (@Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
    (@Atlas.Unitary.IsotropicPoints.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n)
    (@Atlas.Unitary.group_su.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
    (@Atlas.Unitary.specialIsotropicAction.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
DEFINITION Atlas.Unitary.specialIsotropicAction SOURCE Atlas.LinearGroups.Unitary.IsotropicAction
@[instance_reducible] def Atlas.Unitary.specialIsotropicAction.{u_1, u_2} : {F : Type u_1} →
  {K : Type u_2} →
    [inst : Field.{u_1} F] →
      [inst_1 : Field.{u_2} K] →
        [inst_2 : Finite.{u_1 + 1} F] →
          [inst_3 : Finite.{u_2 + 1} K] →
            [inst_4 :
                @Algebra.{u_1, u_2} F K (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
                  (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                    (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))] →
              {n : Nat} →
                (hdeg :
                    @Eq.{1} Nat
                      (@Module.finrank.{u_1, u_2} F K
                        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_1} F
                          (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst)))
                        (@Semiring.toAddCommMonoid.{u_2} K
                          (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                            (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1))))
                        (@Algebra.toModule.{u_1, u_2} F K
                          (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
                          (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                            (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))
                          inst_4))
                      (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 2) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 2)))) →
                  @MulAction.{u_2, u_2} (@Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
                    (@Atlas.Unitary.IsotropicPoints.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n)
                    (@Submonoid.toMonoid.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                      (@Semiring.toMonoid.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                        (@Matrix.semiring.{u_2, 0} (Fin n) K
                          (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                            (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))
                          (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n)))
                      (@Matrix.specialUnitaryGroup.{0, u_2} (Fin n) (instDecidableEqFin n) (Fin.fintype n) K
                        (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1)
                        (@Atlas.Unitary.conjugationStarRing.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 hdeg))) :=
fun {F} {K} [inst : Field.{u_1} F] [inst_1 : Field.{u_2} K] [inst_2 : Finite.{u_1 + 1} F] [inst_3 : Finite.{u_2 + 1} K]
    [inst_4 :
      @Algebra.{u_1, u_2} F K (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
          (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))]
    {n} hdeg =>
  @MulAction.mk.{u_2, u_2} (@Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
    (@Atlas.Unitary.IsotropicPoints.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n)
    (@Submonoid.toMonoid.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
      (@Semiring.toMonoid.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
        (@Matrix.semiring.{u_2, 0} (Fin n) K
          (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
            (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))
          (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n)))
      (@Matrix.specialUnitaryGroup.{0, u_2} (Fin n) (instDecidableEqFin n) (Fin.fintype n) K
        (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1)
        (@Atlas.Unitary.conjugationStarRing.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 hdeg)))
    (@SemigroupAction.mk.{u_2, u_2} (@Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
      (@Atlas.Unitary.IsotropicPoints.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n)
      (@Monoid.toSemigroup.{u_2} (@Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
        (@Submonoid.toMonoid.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
          (@Semiring.toMonoid.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
            (@Matrix.semiring.{u_2, 0} (Fin n) K
              (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))
              (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n)))
          (@Matrix.specialUnitaryGroup.{0, u_2} (Fin n) (instDecidableEqFin n) (Fin.fintype n) K
            (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1)
            (@Atlas.Unitary.conjugationStarRing.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 hdeg))))
      (@SMul.mk.{u_2, u_2} (@Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
        (@Atlas.Unitary.IsotropicPoints.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n) fun g p =>
        @Subtype.mk.{u_2 + 1}
          (@Projectivization.{u_2, u_2} K (Fin n → K) (@Field.toDivisionRing.{u_2} K inst_1)
            (@Pi.addCommGroup.{0, u_2} (Fin n) (fun a => K) fun i =>
              @Ring.toAddCommGroup.{u_2} K (@DivisionRing.toRing.{u_2} K (@Field.toDivisionRing.{u_2} K inst_1)))
            (@Pi.Function.module.{0, u_2, u_2} (Fin n) K K
              (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                (@DivisionRing.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toDivisionRing.{u_2} K inst_1)))
              (@Semiring.toAddCommMonoid.{u_2} K
                (@Ring.toSemiring.{u_2} K (@DivisionRing.toRing.{u_2} K (@Field.toDivisionRing.{u_2} K inst_1))))
              (@Semiring.toModule.{u_2} K
                (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                  (@DivisionRing.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toDivisionRing.{u_2} K inst_1))))))
          (fun p => @Atlas.Unitary.IsotropicLine.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n p)
          (@HSMul.hSMul.{u_2, u_2, u_2} (@Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
            (@Projectivization.{u_2, u_2} K (Fin n → K) (@Field.toDivisionRing.{u_2} K inst_1)
              (@Pi.addCommGroup.{0, u_2} (Fin n) (fun a => K) fun i =>
                @Ring.toAddCommGroup.{u_2} K (@DivisionRing.toRing.{u_2} K (@Field.toDivisionRing.{u_2} K inst_1)))
              (@Pi.Function.module.{0, u_2, u_2} (Fin n) K K
                (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                  (@DivisionRing.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toDivisionRing.{u_2} K inst_1)))
                (@Semiring.toAddCommMonoid.{u_2} K
                  (@Ring.toSemiring.{u_2} K (@DivisionRing.toRing.{u_2} K (@Field.toDivisionRing.{u_2} K inst_1))))

                  (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K

            (@Projectivization.{u_2, u_2} K (Fin n → K) (@Field.toDivisionRing.{u_2} K inst_1)
              (@Pi.addCommGroup.{0, u_2} (Fin n) (fun a => K) fun i =>
                @Ring.toAddCommGroup.{u_2} K (@DivisionRing.toRing.{u_2} K (@Field.toDivisionRing.{u_2} K inst_1)))
              (@Pi.Function.module.{0, u_2, u_2} (Fin n) K K
                (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                  (@DivisionRing.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toDivisionRing.{u_2} K inst_1)))
                (@Semiring.toAddCommMonoid.{u_2} K
                  (@Ring.toSemiring.{u_2} K (@DivisionRing.toRing.{u_2} K (@Field.toDivisionRing.{u_2} K inst_1))))
                (@Semiring.toModule.{u_2} K
                  (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K

            (@instHSMul.{u_2, u_2} (@Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
              (@Projectivization.{u_2, u_2} K (Fin n → K) (@Field.toDivisionRing.{u_2} K inst_1)
                (@Pi.addCommGroup.{0, u_2} (Fin n) (fun a => K) fun i =>
                  @Ring.toAddCommGroup.{u_2} K (@DivisionRing.toRing.{u_2} K (@Field.toDivisionRing.{u_2} K inst_1)))

                  (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                    (@DivisionRing.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toDivisionRing.{u_2} K inst_1)))
                  (@Semiring.toAddCommMonoid.{u_2} K
                    (@Ring.toSemiring.{u_2} K (@DivisionRing.toRing.{u_2} K (@Field.toDivisionRing.{u_2} K inst_1))))

                    (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K

              (@SemigroupAction.toSMul.{u_2, u_2}
                (@Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
                (@Projectivization.{u_2, u_2} K (Fin n → K) (@Field.toDivisionRing.{u_2} K inst_1)
                  (@Pi.addCommGroup.{0, u_2} (Fin n) (fun a => K) fun i =>
                    @Ring.toAddCommGroup.{u_2} K (@DivisionRing.toRing.{u_2} K (@Field.toDivisionRing.{u_2} K inst_1)))
                  (@Pi.Function.module.{0, u_2, u_2} (Fin n) K K
                    (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                      (@DivisionRing.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toDivisionRing.{u_2} K inst_1)))
                    (@Semiring.toAddCommMonoid.{u_2} K

                        (@DivisionRing.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toDivisionRing.{u_2} K inst_1)))

                    (@Semiring.toMonoid.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)

                          (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))

                  (@Projectivization.instMulAction.{u_2, u_2, u_2}
                    (@Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg) K (Fin n → K)
                    (@Pi.addCommGroup.{0, u_2} (Fin n) (fun a => K) fun i =>
                      @Ring.toAddCommGroup.{u_2} K
                        (@DivisionRing.toRing.{u_2} K (@Field.toDivisionRing.{u_2} K inst_1)))

                      (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                        (@DivisionRing.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toDivisionRing.{u_2} K inst_1)))
                      (@Semiring.toAddCommMonoid.{u_2} K
                        (@Ring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                          (@DivisionRing.toRing.{u_2} K (@Field.toDivisionRing.{u_2} K inst_1))))
                      (@Semiring.toModule.{u_2} K
                        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                          (@DivisionRing.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toDivisionRing.{u_2} K inst_1)))))
                    (@Atlas.Unitary.group_su.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
                    (@Atlas.Unitary.specialVectorAction.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg) ⋯))))
            g
            (@Subtype.val.{u_2 + 1}
              (@Projectivization.{u_2, u_2} K (Fin n → K) (@Field.toDivisionRing.{u_2} K inst_1)
                (@Pi.addCommGroup.{0, u_2} (Fin n) (fun a => K) fun i =>
                  @Ring.toAddCommGroup.{u_2} K (@DivisionRing.toRing.{u_2} K (@Field.toDivisionRing.{u_2} K inst_1)))
                (@Pi.Function.module.{0, u_2, u_2} (Fin n) K K
                  (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                    (@DivisionRing.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toDivisionRing.{u_2} K inst_1)))
                  (@Semiring.toAddCommMonoid.{u_2} K
                    (@Ring.toSemiring.{u_2} K (@DivisionRing.toRing.{u_2} K (@Field.toDivisionRing.{u_2} K inst_1))))
                  (@Semiring.toModule.{u_2} K
                    (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                      (@DivisionRing.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toDivisionRing.{u_2} K inst_1))))))
              (fun p => @Atlas.Unitary.IsotropicLine.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n p) p))
          ⋯)
      ⋯)
    ⋯
DEFINITION Atlas.Unitary.specialVectorAction SOURCE Atlas.LinearGroups.Unitary.IsotropicAction
@[instance_reducible] def Atlas.Unitary.specialVectorAction.{u_1, u_2} : {F : Type u_1} →
  {K : Type u_2} →
    [inst : Field.{u_1} F] →
      [inst_1 : Field.{u_2} K] →
        [inst_2 : Finite.{u_1 + 1} F] →
          [inst_3 : Finite.{u_2 + 1} K] →
            [inst_4 :
                @Algebra.{u_1, u_2} F K (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
                  (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                    (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))] →
              {n : Nat} →
                (hdeg :
                    @Eq.{1} Nat
                      (@Module.finrank.{u_1, u_2} F K
                        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_1} F
                          (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst)))
                        (@Semiring.toAddCommMonoid.{u_2} K
                          (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                            (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1))))
                        (@Algebra.toModule.{u_1, u_2} F K
                          (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
                          (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                            (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))
                          inst_4))
                      (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 2) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 2)))) →
                  @DistribMulAction.{u_2, u_2}
                    (@Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg) (Fin n → K)
                    (@Submonoid.toMonoid.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                      (@Semiring.toMonoid.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                        (@Matrix.semiring.{u_2, 0} (Fin n) K
                          (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                            (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))
                          (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n)))
                      (@Matrix.specialUnitaryGroup.{0, u_2} (Fin n) (instDecidableEqFin n) (Fin.fintype n) K
                        (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1)
                        (@Atlas.Unitary.conjugationStarRing.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 hdeg)))
                    (@Pi.addMonoid.{0, u_2} (Fin n) (fun a => K) fun i =>
                      @AddMonoidWithOne.toAddMonoid.{u_2} K
                        (@AddGroupWithOne.toAddMonoidWithOne.{u_2} K
                          (@Ring.toAddGroupWithOne.{u_2} K
                            (@DivisionRing.toRing.{u_2} K (@Field.toDivisionRing.{u_2} K inst_1))))) :=
fun {F} {K} [inst : Field.{u_1} F] [inst_1 : Field.{u_2} K] [inst_2 : Finite.{u_1 + 1} F] [inst_3 : Finite.{u_2 + 1} K]
    [inst_4 :
      @Algebra.{u_1, u_2} F K (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
          (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))]
    {n} hdeg =>
  @DistribMulAction.mk.{u_2, u_2} (@Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg) (Fin n → K)
    (@Submonoid.toMonoid.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
      (@Semiring.toMonoid.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
        (@Matrix.semiring.{u_2, 0} (Fin n) K
          (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
            (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))
          (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n)))
      (@Matrix.specialUnitaryGroup.{0, u_2} (Fin n) (instDecidableEqFin n) (Fin.fintype n) K
        (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1)
        (@Atlas.Unitary.conjugationStarRing.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 hdeg)))
    (@Pi.addMonoid.{0, u_2} (Fin n) (fun a => K) fun i =>
      @AddMonoidWithOne.toAddMonoid.{u_2} K
        (@AddGroupWithOne.toAddMonoidWithOne.{u_2} K
          (@Ring.toAddGroupWithOne.{u_2} K (@DivisionRing.toRing.{u_2} K (@Field.toDivisionRing.{u_2} K inst_1)))))
    (@MulAction.mk.{u_2, u_2} (@Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg) (Fin n → K)
      (@Submonoid.toMonoid.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
        (@Semiring.toMonoid.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
          (@Matrix.semiring.{u_2, 0} (Fin n) K
            (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
              (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))
            (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n)))
        (@Matrix.specialUnitaryGroup.{0, u_2} (Fin n) (instDecidableEqFin n) (Fin.fintype n) K
          (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1)
          (@Atlas.Unitary.conjugationStarRing.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 hdeg)))
      (@SemigroupAction.mk.{u_2, u_2} (@Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
        (Fin n → K)
        (@Monoid.toSemigroup.{u_2} (@Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
          (@Submonoid.toMonoid.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
            (@Semiring.toMonoid.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
              (@Matrix.semiring.{u_2, 0} (Fin n) K
                (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                  (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))
                (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n)))
            (@Matrix.specialUnitaryGroup.{0, u_2} (Fin n) (instDecidableEqFin n) (Fin.fintype n) K
              (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1)
              (@Atlas.Unitary.conjugationStarRing.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 hdeg))))
        (@SMul.mk.{u_2, u_2} (@Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg) (Fin n → K)
          fun g v =>
          @Matrix.mulVec.{u_2, 0, 0} (Fin n) (Fin n) K
            (@NonUnitalNonAssocRing.toNonUnitalNonAssocSemiring.{u_2} K
              (@NonUnitalNonAssocCommRing.toNonUnitalNonAssocRing.{u_2} K
                (@NonUnitalCommRing.toNonUnitalNonAssocCommRing.{u_2} K
                  (@CommRing.toNonUnitalCommRing.{u_2} K (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1)))))
            (Fin.fintype n)
            (@Subtype.val.{u_2 + 1} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
              (fun x =>
                @Membership.mem.{u_2, u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                  (@Submonoid.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                    (@MulZeroOneClass.toMulOneClass.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                      (@instMulZeroOneClassOfSemiring.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                        (@Matrix.semiring.{u_2, 0} (Fin n) K
                          (@CommSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                            (@CommRing.toCommSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1)))
                          (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n)))))
                  (@SetLike.instMembership.{u_2, u_2}
                    (@Submonoid.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                      (@MulZeroOneClass.toMulOneClass.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                        (@instMulZeroOneClassOfSemiring.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                          (@Matrix.semiring.{u_2, 0} (Fin n) K
                            (@CommSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                              (@CommRing.toCommSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1)))
                            (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n)))))
                    (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                    (@Submonoid.instSetLike.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                      (@MulZeroOneClass.toMulOneClass.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                        (@instMulZeroOneClassOfSemiring.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                          (@Matrix.semiring.{u_2, 0} (Fin n) K
                            (@CommSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                              (@CommRing.toCommSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1)))
                            (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n))))))
                  (@Matrix.specialUnitaryGroup.{0, u_2} (Fin n) (instDecidableEqFin n) (Fin.fintype n) K
                    (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1)
                    (@Atlas.Unitary.conjugationStarRing.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 hdeg))
                  x)
              g)
            v)
        ⋯)
      ⋯)
    ⋯ ⋯
DEFINITION Atlas.Unitary.IsotropicLine SOURCE Atlas.LinearGroups.Unitary.IsotropicAction
def Atlas.Unitary.IsotropicLine.{u_3, u_4} : (F : Type u_3) →
  (K : Type u_4) →
    [inst : Field.{u_3} F] →
      [inst_1 : Field.{u_4} K] →
        [Finite.{u_3 + 1} F] →
          [Finite.{u_4 + 1} K] →
            [@Algebra.{u_3, u_4} F K (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_3} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_3} F inst))
                  (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_4} K
                    (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_4} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_4} K inst_1)))] →
              {n : Nat} →
                @Projectivization.{u_4, u_4} K (Fin n → K) (@Field.toDivisionRing.{u_4} K inst_1)
                    (@Pi.addCommGroup.{0, u_4} (Fin n) (fun a => K) fun i =>
                      @Ring.toAddCommGroup.{u_4} K
                        (@DivisionRing.toRing.{u_4} K (@Field.toDivisionRing.{u_4} K inst_1)))
                    (@Pi.Function.module.{0, u_4, u_4} (Fin n) K K
                      (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_4} K
                        (@DivisionRing.toDivisionSemiring.{u_4} K (@Field.toDivisionRing.{u_4} K inst_1)))
                      (@Semiring.toAddCommMonoid.{u_4} K
                        (@Ring.toSemiring.{u_4} K
                          (@DivisionRing.toRing.{u_4} K (@Field.toDivisionRing.{u_4} K inst_1))))
                      (@Semiring.toModule.{u_4} K
                        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_4} K
                          (@DivisionRing.toDivisionSemiring.{u_4} K (@Field.toDivisionRing.{u_4} K inst_1))))) →
                  Prop :=
fun F K [inst : Field.{u_3} F] [inst_1 : Field.{u_4} K] [inst_2 : Finite.{u_3 + 1} F] [inst_3 : Finite.{u_4 + 1} K]
    [inst_4 :
      @Algebra.{u_3, u_4} F K (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_3} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_3} F inst))
        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_4} K
          (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_4} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_4} K inst_1)))]
    {n} p =>
  ∃ v,
    ∃ (hv :
      @Ne.{u_4 + 1} (Fin n → K) v
        (@OfNat.ofNat.{u_4} (Fin n → K) (nat_lit 0)
          (@Zero.toOfNat0.{u_4} (Fin n → K)
            (@Pi.instZero.{0, u_4} (Fin n) (fun a => K) fun i =>
              @MulZeroClass.toZero.{u_4} K
                (@instMulZeroClassOfSemiring.{u_4} K
                  (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_4} K
                    (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_4} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_4} K inst_1)))))))),
      And
        (@Eq.{u_4 + 1}
          (@Projectivization.{u_4, u_4} K (Fin n → K) (@Field.toDivisionRing.{u_4} K inst_1)
            (@Pi.addCommGroup.{0, u_4} (Fin n) (fun a => K) fun i =>
              @Ring.toAddCommGroup.{u_4} K (@DivisionRing.toRing.{u_4} K (@Field.toDivisionRing.{u_4} K inst_1)))
            (@Pi.Function.module.{0, u_4, u_4} (Fin n) K K
              (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_4} K
                (@DivisionRing.toDivisionSemiring.{u_4} K (@Field.toDivisionRing.{u_4} K inst_1)))
              (@Semiring.toAddCommMonoid.{u_4} K
                (@Ring.toSemiring.{u_4} K (@DivisionRing.toRing.{u_4} K (@Field.toDivisionRing.{u_4} K inst_1))))
              (@Semiring.toModule.{u_4} K
                (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_4} K
                  (@DivisionRing.toDivisionSemiring.{u_4} K (@Field.toDivisionRing.{u_4} K inst_1))))))
          p
          (@Projectivization.mk.{u_4, u_4} K (Fin n → K) (@Field.toDivisionRing.{u_4} K inst_1)
            (@Pi.addCommGroup.{0, u_4} (Fin n) (fun a => K) fun i =>
              @Ring.toAddCommGroup.{u_4} K (@DivisionRing.toRing.{u_4} K (@Field.toDivisionRing.{u_4} K inst_1)))
            (@Pi.Function.module.{0, u_4, u_4} (Fin n) K K
              (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_4} K
                (@DivisionRing.toDivisionSemiring.{u_4} K (@Field.toDivisionRing.{u_4} K inst_1)))
              (@Semiring.toAddCommMonoid.{u_4} K
                (@Ring.toSemiring.{u_4} K (@DivisionRing.toRing.{u_4} K (@Field.toDivisionRing.{u_4} K inst_1))))
              (@Semiring.toModule.{u_4} K
                (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_4} K
                  (@DivisionRing.toDivisionSemiring.{u_4} K (@Field.toDivisionRing.{u_4} K inst_1)))))
            v hv))
        (@Eq.{u_4 + 1} K (@Atlas.Unitary.hermitian.{u_3, u_4} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n v v)
          (@OfNat.ofNat.{u_4} K (nat_lit 0)
            (@Zero.toOfNat0.{u_4} K
              (@MulZeroClass.toZero.{u_4} K
                (@instMulZeroClassOfSemiring.{u_4} K
                  (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_4} K
                    (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_4} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_4} K inst_1))))))))

def Atlas.Unitary.IsotropicPoints.{u_3, u_4} : (F : Type u_3) →

    [inst : Field.{u_3} F] →
      [inst_1 : Field.{u_4} K] →
        [Finite.{u_3 + 1} F] →
          [Finite.{u_4 + 1} K] →
            [@Algebra.{u_3, u_4} F K (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_3} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_3} F inst))
