                    (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_4} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_4} K inst_1)))] →
              Nat → Type (max 0 u_4) :=
fun F K [inst : Field.{u_3} F] [inst_1 : Field.{u_4} K] [inst_2 : Finite.{u_3 + 1} F] [inst_3 : Finite.{u_4 + 1} K]
    [inst_4 :
      @Algebra.{u_3, u_4} F K (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_3} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_3} F inst))
        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_4} K
          (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_4} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_4} K inst_1)))]
    n =>
  @Subtype.{u_4 + 1}
    (@Projectivization.{u_4, u_4} K (Fin n → K) (@Field.toDivisionRing.{u_4} K inst_1)
      (@Pi.addCommGroup.{0, u_4} (Fin n) (fun a => K) fun i =>
        @Ring.toAddCommGroup.{u_4} K (@DivisionRing.toRing.{u_4} K (@Field.toDivisionRing.{u_4} K inst_1)))
      (@Pi.Function.module.{0, u_4, u_4} (Fin n) K K
        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_4} K
          (@DivisionRing.toDivisionSemiring.{u_4} K (@Field.toDivisionRing.{u_4} K inst_1)))
        (@Semiring.toAddCommMonoid.{u_4} K
          (@Ring.toSemiring.{u_4} K (@DivisionRing.toRing.{u_4} K (@Field.toDivisionRing.{u_4} K inst_1))))
        (@Semiring.toModule.{u_4} K
          (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_4} K
            (@DivisionRing.toDivisionSemiring.{u_4} K (@Field.toDivisionRing.{u_4} K inst_1))))))
    fun p => @Atlas.Unitary.IsotropicLine.{u_3, u_4} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n p
DEFINITION Atlas.Unitary.scalarSubgroup SOURCE Atlas.LinearGroups.Unitary.ScalarSubgroup
def Atlas.Unitary.scalarSubgroup.{u_1, u_2} : (F : Type u_1) →
  (K : Type u_2) →
    [inst : Field.{u_1} F] →
      [inst_1 : Field.{u_2} K] →
        [inst_2 : Finite.{u_1 + 1} F] →
          [inst_3 : Finite.{u_2 + 1} K] →
            [inst_4 :
                @Algebra.{u_1, u_2} F K (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
                  (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                    (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))] →
              (hdeg :
                  @Eq.{1} Nat
                    (@Module.finrank.{u_1, u_2} F K
                      (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_1} F
                        (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst)))
                      (@Semiring.toAddCommMonoid.{u_2} K
                        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                          (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1))))
                      (@Algebra.toModule.{u_1, u_2} F K
                        (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
                        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                          (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))
                        inst_4))
                    (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 2) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 2)))) →
                (n : Nat) →
                  @Subgroup.{u_2} (@Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
                    (@Atlas.Unitary.group_su.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg) :=
fun F K [inst : Field.{u_1} F] [inst_1 : Field.{u_2} K] [inst_2 : Finite.{u_1 + 1} F] [inst_3 : Finite.{u_2 + 1} K]
    [inst_4 :
      @Algebra.{u_1, u_2} F K (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
          (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))]
    hdeg n =>
  @MonoidHom.range.{u_2, u_2} (↥(@Atlas.Unitary.scalarParameters.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_4 n))
    (@Subgroup.toGroup.{u_2} (↥(@Atlas.FiniteQuadratic.normOne.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_4))
      (@Subgroup.toGroup.{u_2}
        (@Units.{u_2} K
          (@Semiring.toMonoid.{u_2} K
            (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
              (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))))
        (@Units.instGroup.{u_2} K
          (@Semiring.toMonoid.{u_2} K
            (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
              (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))))
        (@Atlas.FiniteQuadratic.normOne.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_4))
      (@Atlas.Unitary.scalarParameters.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_4 n))
    (@Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
    (@Atlas.Unitary.group_su.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
    (@Atlas.Unitary.scalarSpecialHom.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 hdeg n)
DEFINITION Atlas.Unitary.scalarSpecialHom SOURCE Atlas.LinearGroups.Unitary.ScalarSubgroup
def Atlas.Unitary.scalarSpecialHom.{u_1, u_2} : (F : Type u_1) →
  (K : Type u_2) →
    [inst : Field.{u_1} F] →
      [inst_1 : Field.{u_2} K] →
        [inst_2 : Finite.{u_1 + 1} F] →
          [inst_3 : Finite.{u_2 + 1} K] →
            [inst_4 :
                @Algebra.{u_1, u_2} F K (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
                  (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                    (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))] →
              (hdeg :
                  @Eq.{1} Nat
                    (@Module.finrank.{u_1, u_2} F K
                      (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_1} F
                        (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst)))
                      (@Semiring.toAddCommMonoid.{u_2} K
                        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                          (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1))))
                      (@Algebra.toModule.{u_1, u_2} F K
                        (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
                        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                          (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))
                        inst_4))
                    (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 2) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 2)))) →
                (n : Nat) →
                  @MonoidHom.{u_2, u_2} (↥(@Atlas.Unitary.scalarParameters.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_4 n))
                    (@Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
                    (@MulOneClass.toMulOne.{u_2}
                      (↥(@Atlas.Unitary.scalarParameters.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_4 n))
                      (@Monoid.toMulOneClass.{u_2}
                        (↥(@Atlas.Unitary.scalarParameters.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_4 n))
                        (@DivInvMonoid.toMonoid.{u_2}
                          (↥(@Atlas.Unitary.scalarParameters.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_4 n))
                          (@Group.toDivInvMonoid.{u_2}
                            (↥(@Atlas.Unitary.scalarParameters.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_4 n))
                            (@Subgroup.toGroup.{u_2}
                              (↥(@Atlas.FiniteQuadratic.normOne.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_4))
                              (@Subgroup.toGroup.{u_2}
                                (@Units.{u_2} K
                                  (@Semiring.toMonoid.{u_2} K
                                    (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                                      (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))))
                                (@Units.instGroup.{u_2} K
                                  (@Semiring.toMonoid.{u_2} K
                                    (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                                      (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))))
                                (@Atlas.FiniteQuadratic.normOne.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_4))
                              (@Atlas.Unitary.scalarParameters.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_4 n))))))
                    (@MulOneClass.toMulOne.{u_2}
                      (@Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
                      (@Submonoid.toMulOneClass.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                        (@MulZeroOneClass.toMulOneClass.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                          (@instMulZeroOneClassOfSemiring.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                            (@Matrix.semiring.{u_2, 0} (Fin n) K
                              (@CommSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                                (@CommRing.toCommSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1)))
                              (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n))))
                        (@Matrix.specialUnitaryGroup.{0, u_2} (Fin n) (instDecidableEqFin n) (Fin.fintype n) K
                          (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1)
                          (@Atlas.Unitary.conjugationStarRing.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4
                            hdeg)))) :=
fun F K [inst : Field.{u_1} F] [inst_1 : Field.{u_2} K] [inst_2 : Finite.{u_1 + 1} F] [inst_3 : Finite.{u_2 + 1} K]
    [inst_4 :
      @Algebra.{u_1, u_2} F K (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
          (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))]
    hdeg n =>
  @MonoidHom.mk.{u_2, u_2} (↥(@Atlas.Unitary.scalarParameters.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_4 n))
    (@Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
    (@MulOneClass.toMulOne.{u_2} (↥(@Atlas.Unitary.scalarParameters.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_4 n))
      (@Monoid.toMulOneClass.{u_2} (↥(@Atlas.Unitary.scalarParameters.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_4 n))
        (@DivInvMonoid.toMonoid.{u_2} (↥(@Atlas.Unitary.scalarParameters.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_4 n))
          (@Group.toDivInvMonoid.{u_2} (↥(@Atlas.Unitary.scalarParameters.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_4 n))
            (@Subgroup.toGroup.{u_2} (↥(@Atlas.FiniteQuadratic.normOne.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_4))
              (@Subgroup.toGroup.{u_2}
                (@Units.{u_2} K
                  (@Semiring.toMonoid.{u_2} K
                    (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                      (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))))
                (@Units.instGroup.{u_2} K
                  (@Semiring.toMonoid.{u_2} K
                    (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                      (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))))
                (@Atlas.FiniteQuadratic.normOne.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_4))
              (@Atlas.Unitary.scalarParameters.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_4 n))))))
    (@MulOneClass.toMulOne.{u_2} (@Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
      (@Submonoid.toMulOneClass.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
        (@MulZeroOneClass.toMulOneClass.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
          (@instMulZeroOneClassOfSemiring.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
            (@Matrix.semiring.{u_2, 0} (Fin n) K
              (@CommSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K (@CommRing.toCommSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1)))
              (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n))))
        (@Matrix.specialUnitaryGroup.{0, u_2} (Fin n) (instDecidableEqFin n) (Fin.fintype n) K
          (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1)
          (@Atlas.Unitary.conjugationStarRing.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 hdeg))))
    (@OneHom.mk.{u_2, u_2} (↥(@Atlas.Unitary.scalarParameters.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_4 n))
      (@Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
      (@MulOne.toOne.{u_2} (↥(@Atlas.Unitary.scalarParameters.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_4 n))
        (@MulOneClass.toMulOne.{u_2} (↥(@Atlas.Unitary.scalarParameters.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_4 n))
          (@Monoid.toMulOneClass.{u_2} (↥(@Atlas.Unitary.scalarParameters.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_4 n))
            (@DivInvMonoid.toMonoid.{u_2} (↥(@Atlas.Unitary.scalarParameters.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_4 n))
              (@Group.toDivInvMonoid.{u_2} (↥(@Atlas.Unitary.scalarParameters.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_4 n))
                (@Subgroup.toGroup.{u_2} (↥(@Atlas.FiniteQuadratic.normOne.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_4))
                  (@Subgroup.toGroup.{u_2}
                    (@Units.{u_2} K
                      (@Semiring.toMonoid.{u_2} K
                        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                          (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))))
                    (@Units.instGroup.{u_2} K
                      (@Semiring.toMonoid.{u_2} K
                        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                          (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))))
                    (@Atlas.FiniteQuadratic.normOne.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_4))
                  (@Atlas.Unitary.scalarParameters.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_4 n)))))))
      (@MulOne.toOne.{u_2} (@Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
        (@MulOneClass.toMulOne.{u_2} (@Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg)
          (@Submonoid.toMulOneClass.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
            (@MulZeroOneClass.toMulOneClass.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
              (@instMulZeroOneClassOfSemiring.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                (@Matrix.semiring.{u_2, 0} (Fin n) K
                  (@CommSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                    (@CommRing.toCommSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1)))
                  (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n))))
            (@Matrix.specialUnitaryGroup.{0, u_2} (Fin n) (instDecidableEqFin n) (Fin.fintype n) K
              (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1)
              (@Atlas.Unitary.conjugationStarRing.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 hdeg)))))
      (@Atlas.Unitary.scalarSpecial.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 hdeg n) ⋯)
    ⋯
DEFINITION Atlas.Unitary.scalarSpecial SOURCE Atlas.LinearGroups.Unitary.ScalarParameters
def Atlas.Unitary.scalarSpecial.{u_1, u_2} : (F : Type u_1) →
  (K : Type u_2) →
    [inst : Field.{u_1} F] →
      [inst_1 : Field.{u_2} K] →
        [inst_2 : Finite.{u_1 + 1} F] →
          [inst_3 : Finite.{u_2 + 1} K] →
            [inst_4 :
                @Algebra.{u_1, u_2} F K (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
                  (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                    (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))] →
              (hdeg :
                  @Eq.{1} Nat
                    (@Module.finrank.{u_1, u_2} F K
                      (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_1} F
                        (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst)))
                      (@Semiring.toAddCommMonoid.{u_2} K
                        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                          (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1))))
                      (@Algebra.toModule.{u_1, u_2} F K
                        (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
                        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                          (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))
                        inst_4))
                    (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 2) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 2)))) →
                {n : Nat} →
                  ↥(@Atlas.Unitary.scalarParameters.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_4 n) →
                    @Atlas.Unitary.SU.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg :=
fun F K [inst : Field.{u_1} F] [inst_1 : Field.{u_2} K] [inst_2 : Finite.{u_1 + 1} F] [inst_3 : Finite.{u_2 + 1} K]
    [inst_4 :
      @Algebra.{u_1, u_2} F K (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
          (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))]
    hdeg {n} a =>
  @Subtype.mk.{u_2 + 1} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
    (fun x =>
      @Membership.mem.{u_2, u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
        (@Submonoid.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
          (@MulZeroOneClass.toMulOneClass.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
            (@instMulZeroOneClassOfSemiring.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
              (@Matrix.semiring.{u_2, 0} (Fin n) K
                (@CommSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K (@CommRing.toCommSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1)))
                (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n)))))
        (@SetLike.instMembership.{u_2, u_2}
          (@Submonoid.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
            (@MulZeroOneClass.toMulOneClass.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
              (@instMulZeroOneClassOfSemiring.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                (@Matrix.semiring.{u_2, 0} (Fin n) K
                  (@CommSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                    (@CommRing.toCommSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1)))
                  (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n)))))
          (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
          (@Submonoid.instSetLike.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
            (@MulZeroOneClass.toMulOneClass.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
              (@instMulZeroOneClassOfSemiring.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                (@Matrix.semiring.{u_2, 0} (Fin n) K
                  (@CommSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                    (@CommRing.toCommSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1)))
                  (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n))))))
        (@Matrix.specialUnitaryGroup.{0, u_2} (Fin n) (instDecidableEqFin n) (Fin.fintype n) K
          (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1)
          (@Atlas.Unitary.conjugationStarRing.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 hdeg))
        x)
    (@Subtype.val.{u_2 + 1} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
      (fun x =>
        @Membership.mem.{u_2, u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
          (@Submonoid.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
            (@MulZeroOneClass.toMulOneClass.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
              (@instMulZeroOneClassOfSemiring.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                (@Matrix.semiring.{u_2, 0} (Fin n) K
                  (@CommSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                    (@CommRing.toCommSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1)))
                  (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n)))))
          (@SetLike.instMembership.{u_2, u_2}
            (@Submonoid.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
              (@MulZeroOneClass.toMulOneClass.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                (@instMulZeroOneClassOfSemiring.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                  (@Matrix.semiring.{u_2, 0} (Fin n) K
                    (@CommSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                      (@CommRing.toCommSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1)))
                    (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n)))))
            (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
            (@Submonoid.instSetLike.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
              (@MulZeroOneClass.toMulOneClass.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                (@instMulZeroOneClassOfSemiring.{u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
                  (@Matrix.semiring.{u_2, 0} (Fin n) K
                    (@CommSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                      (@CommRing.toCommSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1)))
                    (Fin.fintype n) (instDecidableEqFin n))))))
          (@Matrix.unitaryGroup.{0, u_2} (Fin n) (instDecidableEqFin n) (Fin.fintype n) K
            (@Field.toCommRing.{u_2} K inst_1)
            (@Atlas.Unitary.conjugationStarRing.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 hdeg))
          x)
      (@Atlas.Unitary.diagonalUnitary.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_2 inst_3 inst_4 n hdeg fun x =>
        @Subtype.val.{u_2 + 1} (↥(@Atlas.FiniteQuadratic.normOne.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_4))
          (fun x =>
            @Membership.mem.{u_2, u_2} (↥(@Atlas.FiniteQuadratic.normOne.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_4))
              (@Subgroup.{u_2} (↥(@Atlas.FiniteQuadratic.normOne.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_4))
                (@Subgroup.toGroup.{u_2}
                  (@Units.{u_2} K
                    (@Semiring.toMonoid.{u_2} K
                      (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                        (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))))
                  (@Units.instGroup.{u_2} K
                    (@Semiring.toMonoid.{u_2} K
                      (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                        (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))))
                  (@Atlas.FiniteQuadratic.normOne.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_4)))
              (@SetLike.instMembership.{u_2, u_2}
                (@Subgroup.{u_2} (↥(@Atlas.FiniteQuadratic.normOne.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_4))
                  (@Subgroup.toGroup.{u_2}
                    (@Units.{u_2} K
                      (@Semiring.toMonoid.{u_2} K
                        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                          (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))))
                    (@Units.instGroup.{u_2} K
                      (@Semiring.toMonoid.{u_2} K
                        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                          (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))))
                    (@Atlas.FiniteQuadratic.normOne.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_4)))
                (↥(@Atlas.FiniteQuadratic.normOne.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_4))
                (@Subgroup.instSetLike.{u_2} (↥(@Atlas.FiniteQuadratic.normOne.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_4))
                  (@Subgroup.toGroup.{u_2}
                    (@Units.{u_2} K
                      (@Semiring.toMonoid.{u_2} K
                        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                          (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))))
                    (@Units.instGroup.{u_2} K
                      (@Semiring.toMonoid.{u_2} K
                        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                          (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))))
                    (@Atlas.FiniteQuadratic.normOne.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_4))))
              (@Atlas.Unitary.scalarParameters.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_4 n) x)
          a))
    ⋯
DEFINITION Atlas.Unitary.diagonalUnitary SOURCE Atlas.LinearGroups.Unitary.Determinant
def Atlas.Unitary.diagonalUnitary.{u_1, u_2} : {F : Type u_1} →
  {K : Type u_2} →
    [inst : Field.{u_1} F] →
      [inst_1 : Field.{u_2} K] →
        [inst_2 : Finite.{u_1 + 1} F] →
          [inst_3 : Finite.{u_2 + 1} K] →
            [inst_4 :
                @Algebra.{u_1, u_2} F K (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
                  (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                    (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))] →
              {n : Nat} →
                (hdeg :
                    @Eq.{1} Nat
                      (@Module.finrank.{u_1, u_2} F K
                        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_1} F
                          (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst)))
                        (@Semiring.toAddCommMonoid.{u_2} K
                          (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                            (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1))))
                        (@Algebra.toModule.{u_1, u_2} F K
                          (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
                          (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
                            (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))
                          inst_4))
                      (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 2) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 2)))) →
                  (Fin n → ↥(@Atlas.FiniteQuadratic.normOne.{u_1, u_2} F K inst inst_1 inst_4)) →

    [inst_4 :
      @Algebra.{u_1, u_2} F K (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{u_1} F (@Field.toSemifield.{u_1} F inst))
        (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K
          (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1)))]
    {n} hdeg d =>
  @Subtype.mk.{u_2 + 1} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)
    (fun x =>
      @Membership.mem.{u_2, u_2} (Matrix.{0, 0, u_2} (Fin n) (Fin n) K)

          (@DivisionSemiring.toSemiring.{u_2} K

      fun i =>
      @Units.val.{u_2} K

                  (@Semifield.toDivisionSemiring.{u_2} K (@Field.toSemifield.{u_2} K inst_1))))) :=

    [inst_2 :
